### [1] [Efficient Co-simulator Integration with Application to Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03118)
*Talha Ibn Aziz,Jihoon Og,Ioanis Nikolaidis,Omid Ardakanian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mosaik框架的智能电网高性能协同仿真平台，通过引入基于LBTS的优化事件驱动同步策略，相比传统锁步方法将执行时间缩短高达50%，同时保证了仿真的时序正确性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网作为大规模信息物理系统的典型代表，面临网络攻击等极端事件威胁，高保真协同仿真是研究这些潜在问题的唯一现实策略。然而现有协同仿真方法存在性能瓶颈，亟需开发实用且高性能的协同仿真策略。

Method: 基于Mosaik框架构建协同仿真平台，集成OpenDSS（电力潮流）、改进的NS-3（通信网络）以及自定义Python仿真器（有载调压器控制、分布式状态估计、数据采集）。对比两种同步策略：穷举锁步时间推进与利用下界时间戳（LBTS）和下一事件预测的优化事件驱动方法，并对NS-3事件处理和内部事件相关性过滤进行针对性改进。

Result: 在IEEE 13节点测试馈线系统和扩展的33节点大规模系统（含32个欧洲低压馈线，共1793个节点）上进行性能评估。实验结果表明，基于LBTS的NS-3事件过滤优化事件同步方法相比朴素锁步方法，执行时间减少高达50%（在小规模场景中超过4倍），同时严格保持时序正确性和仿真精度。

Conclusion: 该研究为智能电网等大规模信息物理系统的协同仿真提供了有效的高性能解决方案，所提出的优化同步策略和领域本体标准化方法可推广应用于其他信息物理系统，具有重要的实践意义和参考价值。

Abstract: The evolution of the electric power grid towards a "smart grid" is an example of an emerging large-scale cyber-physical system. To study the potential of cyberattacks or other sources of extreme events, high-fidelity co-simulation is the only realistic strategy. In this work, we demonstrate a practical and high-performance co-simulation strategy for smart grids, which provides lessons about co-simulation integration that can be carried over to other cyber-physical systems. Specifically, we present a co-simulation platform based on the Mosaik framework that integrates several federates including OpenDSS for power flow, a refined NS-3 for communication networks, and custom Python-based simulators for on-load tap changer control, distributed state estimation, and data collection. We compare synchronization strategies -- exhaustive lock-step time advancement versus an optimized event-driven approach that exploits lower-bound time stamps (LBTS) and next-event prediction -- and introduce targeted refinements to NS-3 event handling and relevance filtering of internal events.
  Performance is evaluated on two standard IEEE benchmark systems: the 13-node test feeder with tap-changer voltage regulation and a large-scale 33-bus system augmented with 32 European low-voltage feeders (1,793 nodes total) performing distributed state estimation with thousands of phasors and smart meters. Experimental results demonstrate that the refined event-based synchronization that uses LBTS-based NS-3 event filtering reduces execution time by up to 50% (and more than 4$\times$ in smaller scenarios) compared to naïve lock-step methods, while strictly preserving temporal correctness and simulation accuracy. We additionally summarize a domain ontology that standardizes multi-simulator configuration and entity mapping across power, communication, and control domains.

</details>


### [2] [Feasible but Not Safe: Constraint Violations and Report-Channel Attacks in Learned Cell-Free ISAC Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03147)
*Mehdi Zafari,Iman Mohammadi,A. Lee Swindlehurst*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 基于学习的分布式无蜂窝ISAC调度器存在约束违反和虚假数据攻击脆弱性问题，需要采用可行性投影和一致性检查来确保系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图神经网络(GNN)的调度器假设硬约束在推理时能够满足，且假设统计信息真实可靠，但这些假设在实际部署中可能不成立。研究发现高预测准确率并不能保证联合可行性，且系统对虚假数据注入攻击存在脆弱性。

Method: 以ASSENT算法为例进行实证分析：(1)评估GNN输出对硬约束的违反情况；(2)采用简单的贪心修复过程将GNN输出投影到可行解；(3)分析虚假数据注入攻击的影响，特别是恶意AP报告错误信息的情况；(4)提出低复杂度的跨AP一致性检查机制。

Result: 尽管F1分数很高，许多解仍违反至少一个硬约束；可行性投影能在效用损失很低的情况下恢复约束满足；单个恶意AP报告虚假信息虽不能大幅增加用户关联，但能显著提高不可行解的比例；影响目标函数的错误报告可通过可行性投影缓解，影响约束的错误报告则不能，但后者可通过一致性检查检测。

Conclusion: 学习型ISAC调度器的评估不应仅依赖传统的准确率指标，而应纳入约束感知的可行性度量；可行性投影对于确保约束满足至关重要；需要额外的安全机制来应对虚假数据注入攻击。

Abstract: Learning-based schedulers have been proposed to provide real-time user, target, and access point (AP) association in distributed cell-free integrated sensing and communication systems. In a typical approach, a graph neural network (GNN), trained on labels from a mixed-integer linear program, maps lightweight per-AP statistics to decisions on AP clustering, user and target scheduling, and mode selection in one forward pass. Such solutions assume that hard constraints, enforced only as soft training penalties, hold at inference, and that the self-reported statistics are truthful. Using our ASSENT algorithm as an example, we find that despite high $F_1$ scores, many solutions violate at least one hard constraint, demonstrating that high prediction accuracy does not ensure joint feasibility. Projecting the GNN output onto a feasible solution restores constraint satisfaction with low utility loss, even with a simple greedy repair procedure. We further show that feasibility alone does not guarantee robustness to false data injection attacks. A single malicious AP that reports false information cannot substantially increase its user associations, but can greatly increase the rate of infeasible solutions. The effect of such attacks depends on the type of information being falsified. Misreporting information that affects the objective can largely be mitigated through feasibility projection, whereas falsifying information that affects the constraints cannot. The latter can, however, be detected using a low-complexity cross-AP consistency check. These results show that learned ISAC schedulers should be evaluated using constraint-aware feasibility metrics in addition to conventional accuracy measures.

</details>


### [3] [Bringing dApps to OCUDU: An E3 Controller for Real-Time Open RAN Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03162)
*Angelo Feraudo,Ravis Shirkhani,Stefano Maxenti,Andrea Lacava,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文为Linux基金会的OCUDU项目实现了开源E3Controller，填补了其缺乏E3接口的空白，实现了dApps与RAN的实时交互，且对RAN吞吐量无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open RAN中的实时控制循环越来越多地与gNB共置，dApps通过E3接口实现毫秒级以下的PHY/MAC层信号访问，支持频谱共享、信道感知调度等延迟敏感用例。然而，OCUDU作为Linux基金会的开源CU/DU项目，一直缺乏E3接口实现，限制了dApps在该平台上的部署。

Method: 设计并实现了一个开源E3Controller作为OCUDU的sidecar守护进程。E3协议栈、服务模型逻辑和dApp交互完全运行在RAN核心处理之外，仅通过定义良好的instrumentation hooks与RAN交互。使用Spectrum和L1两种参考服务模型进行验证，通过Foxconn RPQN 4800无线单元将O-RAN前传I/Q样本传递给频谱感知dApp。

Result: 实验评估表明，E3路径对OCUDU RAN没有造成可测量的吞吐量损失，证明了在生产级开源RAN软件上实现实时dApp/RAN接口的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功填补了OCUDU项目中E3接口缺失的技术空白，使dApps能够在生产级开源RAN软件上运行，为AI-RAN生态系统中dApps的广泛部署和可移植性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Real-time control loops in Open RAN are increasingly colocated with the gNB, where dApps, i.e., programmable applications with sub-millisecond access to PHY- and MAC-layer signals, enable latency-critical use cases such as spectrum sharing, channel-aware scheduling, and integrated sensing. What turns dApps from a per-vendor mechanism into a portable component of the emerging AI-RAN ecosystem is the E3 interface: this interface defines how a dApp subscribes to RAN telemetry, receives indications, and issues control actions back to the RAN. To date, E3 has been implemented on OpenAirInterface and on NVIDIA Aerial; OCUDU, i.e., the Linux Foundation's open-source CU/DU project, has lacked a comparable E3 path. We close that gap with an open-source E3Controller for OCUDU, designed as a sidecar daemon so the E3 protocol stack, service-model logic, and dApp interactions live entirely outside RAN core processing, which is touched only through well-defined instrumentation hooks. We validate the controller with two reference service models, Spectrum and L1, that deliver O-RAN fronthaul I/Q samples to a spectrum-sensing dApp over a Foxconn RPQN 4800 radio unit. Our evaluation shows that the E3 path imposes no measurable throughput penalty on the OCUDU RAN, demonstrating that a real-time dApp/RAN interface is feasible on production-grade open RAN software.

</details>


### [4] [Indirect Estimation of SINR via SSB and CSI-RS RSRP in 5G NR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03488)
*Leonardo Spampinato,Mahamadou Togola,Matteo Bernabè,Azim Akhtarshenas,Lorenzo Mario Amorosa,David López-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的方法，仅利用标准化的SSB和CSI-RS参考信号接收功率（RSRP）测量数据，预测5G NR网络中用户设备（UE）的平均下行信干噪比（SINR），并通过基于活跃CSI-RS波束的测量筛选策略显著提升了预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测用户设备（UE）性能对于主动网络控制、资源管理和数字孪生沙盒至关重要，然而基于波束的5G新空口（NR）网络固有的灵活性和复杂性使得准确的性能预测极具挑战性。

Method: 将平均下行SINR预测建模为监督学习问题，仅使用标准化的SSB和CSI-RS参考信号接收功率（RSRP）作为输入特征，在符合3GPP标准的合成数据集上评估多种输入特征表示方法，并提出基于活跃CSI-RS波束的测量筛选策略。

Result: 分析表明，基于活跃CSI-RS波束对测量数据进行筛选能够在降低输入维度的同时显著提升预测准确性，验证了这种活动感知策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了机器学习模型在主动网络优化中的可行性和有效性，为基于标准化参考信号测量的5G网络性能预测提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting user equipment (UE) performance is essential for proactive network control, resource management, and digital twin sandboxes. However, the inherent flexibility and complexity of beam-based 5G new radio (NR) networks make accurate performance forecasting highly challenging. This paper proposes a data-driven approach to predict the average downlink signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) relying exclusively on standardized reference-signal measurements, namely synchronization signal block (SSB) and channel state information-reference signal (CSI-RS) reference signal received power (RSRP). We formulate this prediction as a supervised learning problem and evaluate various input feature representations using a third generation partnership project (3GPP)-compliant synthetic dataset. Our analysis reveals that filtering measurements based on active CSI-RS beams significantly enhances prediction accuracy while reducing input dimensionality. This activity-aware strategy demonstrates the strong viability of machine learning models for proactive network optimization.

</details>


### [5] [A Semantic-Aware Multiple Access Scheme Leveraging Spatial Redundancy for Uplink-Dominant Network Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03559)
*Hamidreza Mazandarani,Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM协议，一种基于多智能体深度强化学习的语义多址接入方案，通过利用用户间的语义空间冗余，实现上行主导网络服务中频谱效率与能耗效率的平衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语义通信技术取得进展，但将语义智能集成到媒体接入控制（MAC）层的研究仍然不足，特别是在利用用户间空间相关性方面。现有方法未能有效挖掘语义感知通信在多址接入中的潜力。

Method: 提出PRISM协议（语义多址接入冗余识别协议），采用无模型多智能体深度强化学习（MADRL），使设备仅凭本地观测即可自主管理频谱接入。引入自吞吐量与辅助吞吐量新指标量化空间冗余，将可变包长接入问题建模为α-公平性与能效优化问题进行分析。

Result: PRISM成功利用语义冗余，性能超越语义无关方案，达到集中式最优基准的90%，在多种用户-语义关联矩阵下实现两个优化目标最高2倍提升。

Conclusion: PRISM验证了语义感知分布式多址接入方案的可行性，可作为未来移动网络应用的候选方案，并可与语义域正交多址方案形成互补。

Abstract: The transition toward semantic-aware communication offers a paradigm shift for next-generation mobile networks, promising to decouple information significance from raw data transmission. Despite advances in semantic extraction, the integration of semantic intelligence into the Medium Access Control (MAC) layer remains underexplored, particularly in exploiting spatial correlations among users. To address this, we introduce a novel multiple access scheme designed for uplink-dominant network services. This framework optimizes the trade-off between spectrum utilization and sustainability by formulating variable-packet-length access as distinct $α$-fairness and energy efficiency problems. A key innovation of our approach is the quantification of spatial redundancies through novel metrics of self-throughput and assisted-throughput, which account for the semantic correlation of data across user equipment. We analyze these formulations to identify optimal bounds before proposing PRISM (Protocol for Redundancy Identification in Semantic Multiple-access). Grounded in Model-free Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning (MADRL), PRISM enables devices to autonomously govern spectrum access using only local observations. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PRISM successfully leverages redundancies to outperform semantic-oblivious schemes, achieving up to \({90\%}\) of the centralized optimal benchmark and improving both objectives by up to \({2\times}\) across diverse user-semantic association matrices. These results validate PRISM as a viable candidate for future distributed mobile network applications, complemented by orthogonal Multiple Access Schemes where signals are multiplexed in the semantic domain.

</details>


### [6] [From Prior-Guided Heuristics to Deployable Agents: Accelerating Demonstration-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Deadline-Constrained Network Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03590)
*Vincenzo Norman Vitale,Mohammad Solki,Antonia Maria Tulino,Andreas F. Molisch,Jaime Llorca*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出MADRL EC(p*)框架和MGA-RL训练协议，通过感知截止时间的拥塞度量和演示驱动的训练方法，解决动态异构网络中端到端峰值延迟保证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NextG交互应用需要在动态异构网络上及时传输延迟敏感信息，但提供严格的端到端峰值延迟保证仍是开放挑战。学习型网络控制面临两大障碍：传统基于流量的路由度量无法捕获流量紧迫性；从零训练的深度强化学习控制器存在样本效率低、训练时间长和早期探索不稳定问题。

Method: 1. 提出有效拥塞(EC)度量族，根据数据包紧迫性量化接口拥塞并主动过滤不可行流量；配合均匀路径分组(UPG)分布启发式促进负载均衡。2. 构建MADRL EC(p*)混合架构，结合分布式调度器和集中式RL路由器。3. 提出统一训练目标，泛化行为克隆、离线RL、在线RL和离线到在线等范式；推导MGA-RL协议，在DDPG基础上实现演示驱动的部署导向训练方法。

Result: EC度量能够感知截止时间并量化流量紧迫性；UPG促进稳健负载均衡；MGA-RL协议通过结合实时奖励、预收集奖励和策略模仿项，提升了样本效率和训练稳定性，相比传统O2O方案具有更好的部署适应性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架同时解决了流量紧迫性感知和训练效率问题，为动态异构网络中的端到端峰值延迟保证提供了可行的学习型网络控制解决方案，具有良好的部署实用性。

Abstract: Timely delivery of delay-sensitive information over dynamic, heterogeneous networks is essential for NextG interactive applications, yet providing strict End-to-End (E2E) peak latency guarantees remains an open challenge. Two obstacles limit the adoption of learning-based network control in this setting: traditional volume-based routing metrics, while highly effective for general traffic management, are not designed to capture traffic urgency; and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) controllers trained from scratch suffer from sample inefficiency, long training times, and early-stage exploration volatility. This paper introduces a deployment-focused network control framework that addresses both obstacles. First, we present Effective Congestion (EC), a deadline-aware metric family that quantifies interface congestion by packet urgency and proactively filters non-viable traffic, coupled with a Uniform Path Grouping (UPG) distribution heuristic promoting robust load-balancing; the resulting policies are embedded into Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning Effective Congestion ($p^*$) (MADRL EC ($p^*$)), a hybrid architecture combining a distributed scheduler with a centralized RL-based router. Second, we introduce a unified training objective that generalizes existing policy-learning paradigms---behavioral cloning, offline Reinforcement Learning (RL), online RL, and offline-to-online schemes---as special cases, combining a live-reward term, a pre-collected-reward term, and a policy-imitation term. From this objective, we derive the Model-Guided Annealed Reinforcement Learning (MGA-RL) protocol, instantiated on a Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) backbone: a deployment-oriented, demonstration-driven training approach that generalizes conventional Offline-to-Online (O2O) schemes, in which trajectories from a lightweight [...]

</details>


### [7] [Employing the Structural Power to Achieve Supply-Demand Balanced Payment Channel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03600)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Hongwei Shi,Weicheng Wang,Anlin Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑优化的支付通道网络(PCN)流动性分配方法，通过引入支付拓扑熵(PTE)指标和MaxPTE算法，在不增加额外资金的情况下，仅通过重新配置网络拓扑结构即可显著减少供需冲突，提升网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链支付通道网络面临供需不匹配问题：通道预存余额需要匹配交易需求，余额不足会导致交易失败。现有方法如交易路径优化和通道余额分配需要动态适应波动的需求，成本高昂。研究发现了PCN中余额短缺与网络拓扑结构的相关性，为从静态拓扑角度解决供需冲突提供了新思路。

Method: 首先揭示了PCN中余额短缺与网络拓扑结构的相关性，发现了流动性分配的结构组织规律。提出了支付拓扑熵(PTE)指标，这是一个基于信息论的度量，量化每个节点相对于全局平均连接模式的偏差，捕获余额供给的结构特性。在此基础上设计了MaxPTE拓扑优化算法，通过结构重配置重新组织通道间的余额分配，使静态余额分布与动态交易需求相匹配，无需额外的余额补充。

Result: 实验结果表明，MaxPTE算法相比现有基准方法：(1)减少余额短缺27.25%；(2)平均最大流增加12.23%；(3)交易失败概率降低25.96%。该方法在不同余额-需求分布下保持鲁棒性，无需预先预测需求即可改善供需平衡。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑优化方法可以从静态角度有效解决PCN的供需冲突问题，避免了现有动态适应方法的成本问题。所提出的PTE指标和MaxPTE算法为PCN流动性管理提供了新的理论基础和实践方案，证明了在不增加额外资金的情况下，仅通过网络结构重配置即可显著改善网络性能。

Abstract: Blockchain technology faces scalability challenges because transactions must be validated and recorded across the network. Payment channel networks (PCNs) improve efficiency by moving transactions off-chain and recording only critical interactions on the mainnet. However, PCNs require pre-deposited channel balances (supply) to match transaction demands (demand), and insufficient balances cause supply shortages. Existing approaches, including transaction path optimization and channel balance allocation, address this problem but incur high costs due to dynamic adaptation to fluctuating demands. We reveal a correlation between balance deficits in PCNs and network topology by uncovering the structural organization of liquidity allocation. This enables supply-demand conflicts to be mitigated from a static topological perspective through channel reconfiguration, without additional balance replenishment. We further introduce payment topological entropy (PTE), an information-theoretic metric that quantifies each node's deviation from the global average connection pattern and captures structural properties of balance supply. Based on PTE, we design MaxPTE, a topology optimization algorithm that reorganizes balance allocation across channels through structural reconfiguration, aligning static balance distribution with dynamic transaction demand. Extensive experiments show that MaxPTE reduces balance deficits by 27.25%, increases average maximum flow by 12.23%, and decreases transaction failure probability by 25.96%, outperforming existing benchmarks. The method also remains robust across diverse balance-demand distributions, improving supply-demand balance without prior demand prediction.

</details>


### [8] [Is Collision-Free Backoff Worth It in Wi-Fi?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03817)
*Mohammad Yousefi,Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究无碰撞退避算法在Wi-Fi中实际非全缓冲流量下的性能表现，发现其在全缓冲流量下吞吐量提升有限（最高6.3%），在非全缓冲流量下甚至可能导致性能下降，质疑了无碰撞方法在现实场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi中的分布式协调功能（DCF）在高竞争情况下因碰撞导致性能下降。无碰撞退避算法通过确定性信道访问取代随机化，理论上可提高效率和可预测性。然而，尚不清楚这些方法在现实非全缓冲流量（包含上下行传输）场景下是否真正能提升Wi-Fi性能。

Method: 通过系统级仿真对比三种无碰撞算法（CSMA/ECA、CSMA/E2CA、确定性退避DetBO）与标准及单阶段BEB的性能。评估场景包括：全缓冲流量、非全缓冲ON/OFF流量（上下行传输）、与遗留BEB站点共存。

Result: 在全缓冲流量下，无碰撞操作仅提供有限的吞吐量增益（最高6.3%）。在非全缓冲流量下，延迟主要由竞争窗口（CW）而非碰撞避免决定，且在下行密集型流量下无碰撞访问甚至可能导致性能下降。站点至少93%的时间回退到随机退避，因此无碰撞调度很少在AP之外形成。

Conclusion: 无碰撞退避算法在现实Wi-Fi场景中的效果有限。在全缓冲流量下增益微小，在非全缓冲流量下性能可能恶化，且无碰撞调度难以在实际环境中维持。这表明仅依靠无碰撞方法不足以显著改善Wi-Fi在真实流量模式下的性能。

Abstract: The Distributed Coordination Function (DCF)---the underlying channel access protocol in Wi-Fi, based on Carrier Sense Multiple Access with Collision Avoidance (CSMA/CA) and Binary Exponential Backoff (BEB)---is simple and effective, but its performance can degrade with increasing contention due to collisions. Collision-free backoff algorithms replace randomization with deterministic channel access, potentially improving efficiency and predictability. But are collision-free methods, with all their implications in terms of protocol design, sufficient to improve Wi-Fi performance under realistic non-full-buffer traffic with both uplink and downlink transmissions? We investigate this question by comparing three collision-free algorithms, CSMA/ECA (ECA), CSMA/E2CA (E2CA), and deterministic backoff (DetBO), with standard and single-stage BEB. Using system-level simulations, we evaluate full-buffer traffic, non-full-buffer ON/OFF traffic with both uplink and downlink transmissions, and coexistence with legacy BEB stations. Under full-buffer traffic, collision-free operation provides only modest throughput gains, up to 6.3\%. With non-full-buffer traffic, delay is governed primarily by the contention window (CW) rather than by collision avoidance, and collision-free access can even degrade performance under downlink-heavy traffic. Stations fall back to a random backoff at least 93\% of the time, so a collision-free schedule rarely forms outside the AP.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [9] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在不确定时依赖一个“无知方向”作为贝叶斯先验，该方向编码了训练语料的单字分布，并可通过调控该方向主动引导模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型在缺乏上下文线索时的预测行为，揭示其如何利用训练语料的单字分布作为先验知识来应对不确定性。

Method: 在四个模型家族中识别出嵌入矩阵中的一个特定方向（即“无知方向”），通过投影最终预测状态到该方向计算先验载荷因子λ，建立几何-概率解释框架，并验证其因果作用。

Result: 在所有被测试模型中均发现了该“无知方向”，λ随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降；模型大小越大，在高上下文极限下的先验依赖性越低；主动调控λ可有效引导预测朝向或远离单字先验。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测可分解为正交的两个向量：由λ调控的单字先验和上下文驱动的似然，这为理解和控制模型的不确定性预测提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [10] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方程重构方法，将参数化算子学习转化为单一规范算子学习，实现跨参数域的零样本预测和有效预警机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纯数据驱动参数化模型在学习宽参数范围的解算子时，需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数，且在训练分布之外可能静默失效。

Method: 引入方程重构技术，从控制方程解析推导参数诱导的算子变化，将其吸收为有效源项，从而将参数化算子学习重新表述为学习单一规范算子。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE设置中，该方法支持外推预测，能够在共享规范表示中整合稀疏异构数据集，并将收敛损失作为重构迭代失效的内部预警信号。在核聚变托卡马克高保真模拟中，该框架通过规范域映射统一了四种装置几何的电子温度数据。

Conclusion: 方程重构为构建可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条新途径，该方法结合了方程引导的迁移、数据高效性和可监测推理能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [11] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统性地回顾了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习研究，提出了图数据场景的分类体系、统计异质性特征及标准化问题框架，并指出了未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式机器学习面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制；现有协作学习研究主要关注欧几里得数据，无法捕捉现实世界中普遍存在的图结构数据的关联模式；图学习中的消息传递机制天然适合协作环境，但图结构数据在协作学习中的机遇与挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 首先回顾欧几里得数据协作学习的基础原理，沿三个核心维度（学习有效性、效率、隐私保护）进行组织；然后将讨论扩展到图结构数据，引入图分布场景的分类体系，刻画相关统计异质性，并开发标准化问题表述和算法框架。

Result: 建立了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据协作学习的全面调查框架；提出了图分布场景的分类法；表征了相关的统计异质性；开发了标准化问题表述和算法框架；系统性地识别了开放性挑战和有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文为协作学习从欧几里得数据扩展到图结构数据提供了系统性基础，通过建立分类体系、问题框架和算法体系，巩固了这一新兴研究领域，为后续研究提供了清晰的路线图和参考基准。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [12] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过RFIS和RPD两个干预指标，在RoPE类Transformer中发现了一种基于频率的头级别功能分类：全局检索头和局部位置头，并提出了头级混合架构（HwH），在保持性能的同时显著提升了长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全注意力（FA）和线性注意力（LA）混合架构分配方式仍停留在启发式层面，缺乏理论依据。作者旨在通过研究RoPE类Transformer中注意力头的功能性组织，为混合架构的设计提供基于实证的指导原则。

Method: 提出了两个干预指标：RoPE频率重要性评分（RFIS）衡量各频率对注意力分布的影响，RoPE位置依赖性（RPD）隔离旋转位置编码调制的影响。在Qwen3和Llama3.1模型上分析，识别出由训练长度位置尺度决定的全局位置边界（GPBand）。据此提出头级混合架构（HwH），使用NoPE FA进行全局检索，LA进行局部位置建模。

Result: RFIS和RPD验证了完整的检索头与位置头分类，通过中低频带清晰分隔。HwH架构的FA与LA比例低于1:3，在保持语言建模和常识推理性能的同时，改善了检索能力，并在零样本长上下文外推任务上显著超越Transformer、纯LA和层级混合基线模型。消融实验验证了设计原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 位置建模应仅在局部进行，全局访问应通过位置无关的检索实现；两种功能应在头粒度上分配并采用层特定的分配策略。这种有原则的混合架构设计为未来基础模型的发展提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [13] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，直接在TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散方程的基本未知量，实现了跨尺寸泛化和快速器件设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD仿真在新兴FinFET器件设计中不可或缺，但计算成本极高，尤其是3D结构的运行时随网格复杂度急剧增加，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理模型将固定设计参数映射到少量标量指标，丢失了底层物理机制，在器件几何结构和系列间缺乏可迁移性。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型：1）直接在四面体TCAD网格上运行，预测每个网格节点的静电势、电子和空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量）；2）训练结合数据损失和有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数；3）采用深度集成方法的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习循环，快速筛选候选设计池。

Result: 在多鳍三栅FinFET上与Sentaurus Device对比验证：1）三个漂移-扩散场预测RMSE低于每场1V；2）逐设计吞吐量比完整模拟器高出多个数量级；3）在大型多鳍阵列上推理仍能在1秒内完成；4）模型在小鳍数量网格上训练后可直接应用于更大阵列，仅受GPU内存限制。

Conclusion: 该代理模型实现了尺寸泛化能力，使得直接TCAD扫描无法实现的大规模器件Pareto前沿探索成为可能，显著加速了FinFET器件设计流程，为跨尺度器件优化提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [14] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailRL方法，通过优化奖励分布的上尾概率而非平均奖励，使策略更关注罕见高奖励样本，在多个任务上避免次优解并提升推理时的采样效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，掩盖了不同策略在产生罕见高奖励结果概率上的差异。随着训练和推理中采样的增加，保留高奖励结果的概率质量对模型性能至关重要，而平均奖励无法直接优化这一特性。

Method: 提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)：将连续奖励转化为一系列二元成功事件（是否超过各奖励阈值），最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率。通过修改优势函数实现，可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合，给予罕见高奖励rollout更大权重。

Result: 在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI定位和代码优化四个任务上，TailRL利用罕见高奖励训练样本避免次优解，训练出的模型在推理时能从额外采样中获益更多。

Conclusion: TailRL仅需简单修改优势函数即可兼容现有RL流水线，通过直接优化尾概率覆盖而非期望奖励，有效解决了传统方法忽视罕见高奖励事件的问题，提升了策略对高奖励结果的探索和利用能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [15] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeanGRPO方法，通过重构数据并行布局和引入两种无需重计算的训练调度（LeanGRPO-Retain和LeanGRPO-Reweight），解决了扩散强化学习中冗余重计算问题，实现高达1.83倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散强化学习方法（如DanceGRPO和FlowGRPO）在rollout后需要对选定时间步进行带梯度的重计算，在on-policy训练场景下这种重计算是数学上冗余的。虽然直觉上可以复用前向传播骨干网络，但这会导致rollout期间的大量内存开销。

Method: 通过重构数据并行布局，提出两种无需重计算的trajectory-logprob扩散RL训练调度：(1) LeanGRPO-Retain在rollout期间启用梯度跟踪，直接复用计算图和保存的激活进行反向传播，无需重计算；(2) LeanGRPO-Reweight在rollout期间启用梯度，使用临时优势值立即反向传播每个选定步骤并延迟梯度同步，在轨迹完成后用真实优势值校正临时梯度。两种调度针对不同的模型规模和输入大小。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO框架下，结合FLUX.1-dev和Wan模型，LeanGRPO实现了高达1.83倍的端到端加速，同时保持了原始优化目标不变。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO成功消除了扩散强化学习中的冗余计算，通过创新的训练调度策略在保持优化目标的前提下显著提升了训练效率，为大规模扩散模型的强化学习训练提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [16] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种基于图网络的颗粒动力学模拟器，通过在接触边上存储交互历史，在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，相比GNS和NMGNS将长时预测位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时实现比MPM求解器快8.9-12.2倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒运动强烈依赖于颗粒间接触历史，但现有模拟器主要将时序信息存储在节点特征或节点级内存中，难以在粒子接触形成、断裂和重排时有效保持历史信息。

Method: TRACE在每个接触边上维护持久内存，通过注意力机制消息传递和门控循环单元(GRU)更新，使用边身份字典在接触图变化时保持该内存。物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力，执行库仑摩擦极限，并施加大小相等方向相反的内力。模型采用单步预训练和自回归展开微调的训练策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍塌基准测试中，TRACE产生稳定、物理一致的长时预测，准确复现最终沉积几何形态和坍塌过程中释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，长时位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，参数更少且保持近零颗粒相互渗透。相比MPM参考求解器，在2D和3D分别实现12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过将交互历史直接存储在接触边上，TRACE有效解决了颗粒动力学中接触历史保持的难题，实现了更准确、高效且物理一致的长期预测，为高保真求解器提供了高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [17] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLIWBO方法，通过有限库输入扭曲解决GP-UCB收敛保证与输入变形之间的矛盾，在几何失配场景下显著提升性能且保持理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GP-UCB方法需要固定核函数才能保证收敛性，但核函数编码了输入邻近性与目标值相似性的关系。当原始坐标与几何结构不匹配时（如对数缩放的超参数或局部峰值），输入扭曲可大幅提高采样效率，然而已知的GP-UCB证明要求固定核函数，这形成了理论与实际应用之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），从有限的平滑输入映射库中选择扭曲函数，可采用任意历史依赖规则进行选择。该方法通过有限库规模代价（√N_ε）在温和假设下保持高概率收敛保证，同时自适应调整输入几何结构以加速学习。

Result: 在四个重复基准测试（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST HPO）中，FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配情况下优于原始坐标GP-UCB；能够逃脱连oracle扭曲期望改进都无法解决的陷阱；恢复手动对数缩放的大部分增益；在所有具有匹配遗憾保证的测试方法中表现最佳。20维MAS设计研究验证了在昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO成功解决了输入扭曲与收敛保证之间的理论冲突，在保持理论保证的同时实现了显著性能提升，为GP-BO在几何失配场景下的应用提供了有效解决方案。有限库设计是平衡理论保证与实践性能的关键创新。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [18] [An Adversarial Zero-Shot Learning Approach for Anomaly Detection in Multivariate IoT Traffic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03505)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Tolunay Seyfi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于序列VAE的对抗对比学习框架，实现IoT网络异常检测的零样本域自适应，在44个跨域场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT网络异常检测面临设备多样性、标签数据缺失和环境域变化等独特挑战，传统方法难以在异构部署条件下有效泛化。

Method: 构建序列VAE架构，结合对抗学习与对比损失优化域不变潜在表示；引入编码器/解码器适配层对齐跨域特征分布；提出基于目的地的分割策略建模IoT通信结构，实现无需标签数据或原始特征迁移的零样本域自适应。

Result: 在覆盖工业、企业、通用、智能家居、军事自动化等6个数据集的44个迁移场景中，展现出强零样本泛化能力，并在异构、隐私约束的IoT条件下与对比域自适应基线达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了IoT网络异常检测中的域适应问题，在无标签和隐私保护场景下实现了良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in Internet of Things (IoT) networks presents unique challenges due to the diversity of devices, lack of labeled data, and domain variability across environments. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection that leverages adversarial learning and contrastive loss within a sequence-based Variational Autoencoder (VAE) architecture. Our method enables zero-shot domain adaptation by jointly optimizing domain-invariant latent representations and semantically structured embedding spaces, without requiring labeled data or raw feature transfer. To address the heterogeneity of IoT deployments, we introduce encoder and decoder adaptor layers that align feature distributions across domains while preserving contextual semantics. Additionally, we propose a destination-based segmentation strategy to better model real-world communication structures in IoT traffic. Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [19] [Risk and Anomaly Identification for Distribution Network Optimal Operation Based on Reinforcement Learning and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03308)
*Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个显式感知不确定性的深度强化学习框架，通过二阶不确定性量化区分固有风险和分布外异常，实现配电网络的可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代配电网络的可靠运行需要在普遍不确定性下及时识别运行风险和异常事件。实际操作中，运维人员必须识别随机但分布内的固有风险，以及由异常负荷模式、极端天气或网络物理攻击引起的分布外异常行为。

Method: 整合分布式深度强化学习和贝叶斯深度强化学习，构建二阶不确定性量化方案，将总不确定性分解为偶然不确定性（aleatoric）和认知不确定性（epistemic）。前者用于刻画固有运行风险，后者用于训练阶段的探索和部署阶段的分布外检测及回退控制。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的深度强化学习智能体具有良好的性能，不确定性量化方法能够有效区分并识别固有风险和分布外异常。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了配电网络中风险识别与异常检测的联合任务，为不确定性环境下的配电网络最优运行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable operation of modern distribution networks requires timely identification of operational risks and anomalous events under pervasive uncertainty. In practice, operators must identify risks that are inherent in stochastic yet in-distribution conditions, and anomalies that correspond to out-of-distribution behaviors such as unusual load patterns, extreme weather or cyber-physical attacks. This paper addresses this joint risk and anomaly identification problem for optimal distribution network operation and proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework that is explicitly uncertainty aware. We integrate distributional and Bayesian deep reinforcement learning to realize a second- order uncertainty quantification scheme that decomposes total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, which are respectively used to characterize inherent risk and out-of- distribution anomalies. The resulting epistemic estimates drive both exploration during training and out-of-distribution detec- tion with fallback control during deployment, whereas aleatoric estimates are used to characterize intrinsic operational risk. Simulation results demonstrate the performance of our DRL agent and the effectiveness of the uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [20] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出将电子航海图（ENC）矢量数据表示为图结构，利用图神经网络（GNN）对ENC变更进行安全性风险分类，实验证明图表示方法能够有效提升分类准确性，为自动化ENC维护提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子航海图（ENC）作为海上导航使用的地理空间矢量数据集，其维护工作面临挑战，特别是对ENC对象变更按照对航行安全的重要性进行分类尤为关键。尽管图神经网络（GNN）适合图结构数据学习，但尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合ENC变更分类任务。

Method: 研究将矢量导航数据集表示为图结构，其中空间对象作为节点，其空间和语义关系构成边。将旧ENC数据集和新ENC数据集编码为一对图，将任务框架化为图对分类问题。基于此表示，研究多种GNN架构和模型配置，用于分类编码图是否构成对航行安全的关键或非关键风险，并在海事专家审核的ENC变更数据上进行训练和评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示方法能够改进ENC更新的分类效果，提供了可扩展的方法来自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程。多种GNN架构在专家审核的ENC变更数据集上进行了评估验证。

Conclusion: 图神经网络应用于ENC变更分类是可行且有效的，图表示方法相比传统方法能够更好地捕捉ENC数据中的空间和语义关系，为航海图维护自动化提供了新的技术路径，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [21] [Inferred Generative-Process Diversity Predicts Correlated Failure Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03422)
*Ross Tieman,Evan Markou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算法信息理论的生成过程多样性度量方法，用于评估语言模型群体的多样性。研究发现，该方法能捕捉到语义相似性无法识别的模型群体结构，并能预测模型对之间的相关性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型群体的多样性评估主要依赖语义相似性，这仅能捕捉输出含义的差异。然而，不同模型可能被当作独立组件处理，即使它们的行为和故障仍然高度相关。因此需要一种更基础的多样性概念来区分能够生成观察输出的不同过程。

Method: 基于算法信息理论，使用原始模型输出之间的归一化压缩距离（Normalised Compression Distance），并通过置换控制进行残差化处理，作为推断生成过程多样性的度量方法。研究覆盖38个语言模型和10个不相交的基准测试家族。

Result: 该度量方法识别出了语义相似性所忽略的群体结构，并预测了模型对在跨任务中的机会校正相关性失败变化。跨基准部分等级相关性为-0.216（95%置信区间[-0.309,-0.122]），在所有10个基准上均为负值。这表明增加的生成过程多样性与减少的相关性失败相关，而这种关联不能归因于语义相似性或能力。

Conclusion: 推断的生成过程多样性为研究安全相关语境中多模型系统的多样性提供了一种新颖且实用的方法，有助于更准确地评估语言模型群体的独立性和韧性。

Abstract: Diversity is a widely observed factor in the resilient function of collective systems, yet the type of diversity that matters depends on the properties and failure modes of the system. This distinction is important for systems composed of multiple language models. Different models may be treated as independent components even when their behaviour and failures remain strongly correlated. Assessments of language-model populations using semantic similarity demonstrate limited semantic diversity, but this captures only differences in the meaning of observed outputs. We argue that a more fundamental notion of model diversity is generative-process diversity, the differences between processes capable of generating the observed outputs. Drawing from Algorithmic Information Theory, we use Normalised Compression Distance between raw model outputs, residualised against a permutation control, as a measure of inferred generative-process diversity. Across 38 language models, this measure identifies population structure missed by semantic similarity and predicts cross-task variation in chance-corrected correlated failure among model pairs across ten disjoint benchmark families, beyond semantic similarity and model-pair capability. The cross-benchmark partial rank association is $-0.216$ with a 95% interval of $[-0.309,-0.122]$, and the estimate is negative on all ten benchmarks. These results indicate that increased generative-process diversity is associated with reduced correlated failure in model pairs that is not attributable to semantic similarity or capability. Inferred generative-process diversity offers a novel and practical approach for investigating diversity of multi-model systems in safety-relevant contexts.

</details>


### [22] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD)，通过在提示级别验证教师可靠性来过滤不可靠的蒸馏信号，显著提升了蒸馏效果并大幅提高了教师节点GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的On-Policy Distillation (OPD)对所有提示均匀应用教师监督，但教师在某些提示上可能不可靠。由于反向KL散度具有模态搜索特性，自信但错误的教师会产生强烈的误导性更新。现有的熵或教师-学生似然一致性等分布代理指标无法直接验证结果的正确性。

Method: TGOPD在允许密集监督之前，在提示级别验证教师可靠性。具体方法：(1) 从一小部分验证器评分的教师探针中估计可靠性；(2) 根据可靠性检查结果路由每个提示——通过检查则进行密集OPD，否则使用验证器引导的GRPO；(3) 利用原本闲置的教师容量进行可靠性估计。

Result: 在4B和35B参数规模的学生模型上，涵盖数学、代码和指令遵循任务，TGOPD在所有六个单域设置中均优于标准OPD。在多域训练中，两种规模下均实现了更高的七基准平均分。同时将教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（在4B单域实验中）。

Conclusion: TGOPD通过提示级别的教师可靠性验证，有效解决了教师不可靠导致的误导性更新问题，在多个领域和规模上实现了性能提升，同时大幅提高了异步OPD中教师侧的计算效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [23] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新兴的因果推理范式——因果基础模型（CFMs），通过预训练神经网络实现无需微调的因果推断，为传统需要定制流程的因果推理提供了通用化解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推理需要针对每个新问题构建定制化流程：提出因果机制、选择兼容估计器并训练模型。而机器学习领域已转向基础模型范式（大规模预训练后直接应用于新任务），本文旨在将这一范式引入因果推理领域，解决传统方法需要针对每个问题重新设计流程的低效问题。

Method: 提出因果基础模型（CFMs），即预训练神经网络，通过上下文学习在全新数据集上估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需模型更新。提供因果推理和机器学习必要背景知识，并附带示例代码和Jupyter notebooks进行实践演示。

Result: 提供了对因果基础模型这一新兴领域的实用介绍，总结了CFMs的核心概念和工作原理，为研究者提供了实践入门指南和可执行代码示例。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型代表了因果推理的新发展方向，通过预训练和上下文学习实现了因果推断的通用化和便捷化，有望改变传统因果推理需要定制化流程的现状，推动该领域向基础模型范式转变。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [24] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ObserverBench基准框架，用于测试内部估计器（观察者）是否适用于其指导的干预、控制或安全任务，证明了仅靠估计准确度是不够的，需同时考虑动作选择导致的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性越来越多地用于指导干预（如激活引导、电路移除、安全监控），但一个平均准确的内部估计仍可能导致不良动作，因此需要评估观察者是否真正适合其所指导的任务。

Method: 提出ObserverBench基准框架，每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、保留案例和损失，分别报告估计准确度和所选动作导致的损失。在GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it等模型上进行电路干预和安全分诊实验。

Result: 实验显示：1）成对观察者在预测未见效果时更准确，但不总是选择更好的动作；2）在动作损失上训练的观察者选择损失更低的动作；3）完美分离违规的安全分数在违规成本不同时仍可能分配不当；4）AUROC排名可能与部署损失排名不同；5）稀疏SAE读取结果落后于其层匹配的密集控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench通过固定任务契约、可运行基线和基于表格的提交，为评估可解释性方法通过其所启用的动作提供了有效工具，证明了估计准确度和动作损失都需要被考虑。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [25] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了针对具有转移不确定性的广义和并发随机博弈（CSGs）的PAC学习框架，能够学习社会最优的纳什均衡或证明其不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有转移不确定性的广义和并发随机博弈中，纳什均衡的存在性是一个关键挑战。现有的学习方法无法在理论保证下同时处理均衡学习与存在性判定问题，因此需要一个统一框架来解决这一难题。

Method: 算法维护基于数据的L¹置信集来估计转移核，通过求解鲁棒CSG计算社会最优的ε-纳什均衡。引入纳什边际刻画实现对均衡存在性的原则性推理：框架要么返回ε-近似NE，要么提供可靠的不存在性证明。采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R²_max H⁴ |S|² |A| / (p_reach ε²))。在基准CSG上的实验验证了近最优性能、正确处理均衡存在/不存在情况，以及与理论一致的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架首次为转移不确定的CSG提供了PAC保证的均衡学习方法，并能可靠地判定纳什均衡是否存在，填补了多智能体学习理论中的重要空白。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


### [26] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM通过局部预训练和组合级迁移学习，显著降低了神经算子在新几何/条件下的部署成本，在Darcy流动和俯仰翼型流场预测中误差降低36-56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为物理仿真的快速可微分替代模型，在域几何、尺寸或运行条件与训练数据不同时精度会下降。监督适应可以恢复精度，但即使是小型目标集也需要昂贵的高保真仿真。作者希望协同设计预训练和迁移学习，以降低此部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM首先在小子域上预训练神经算子来预测场量。对于新设置，冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测。这种协同设计的局部预训练和组合级迁移策略避免了为每个新场景重新训练完整模型。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流动问题中，使用16个目标仿真进行适应后，在更大的域上误差比处理整个域的容量匹配模型低36-56%。在快速俯仰翼型绕流中，无论是零样本还是少样本校准，都改善了20步场量预测结果。成功处理了跨更大孔隙介质域的长程压力耦合和超出训练范围的俯仰频率问题。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练和组合级迁移学习被确定为物理基础模型的有前景设计原则。LatentDDM为降低神经算子在多样化部署场景下的成本提供了有效解决方案，在保持精度的同时显著减少了高保真仿真需求。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [27] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream提出流式推测-修正框架，通过利用部分GPU结果逐步优化计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，在移动和嵌入式平台上将内存使用降低4.8-7.5倍，同时提升吞吐量1.6-2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理在隐私保护和响应速度方面具有吸引力，但在移动和嵌入式设备上面临模型权重远超可用DRAM容量的挑战。现有系统利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载到SSD或闪存存储，但面临根本性的系统权衡：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要早期预测，导致现有设计要么序列化执行，要么产生冗余权重获取、额外计算和巨大缓存开销。

Method: 提出LeanStream，一个流式推测-修正框架。该方法使用部分GPU结果逐步修正计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O之间的细粒度重叠，从而解决准确决策与早期预测之间的矛盾。

Result: 在移动和嵌入式平台上实现并评估LeanStream。与现有设备端LLM推理系统相比，在达到先前工作最佳吞吐量的条件下，LeanStream将内存使用量降低4.8倍至7.5倍，同时将token生成吞吐量进一步提升1.6倍至2.1倍。

Conclusion: LeanStream成功解决了设备端LLM推理中计算-I/O权衡问题，通过流式推测-修正方法显著降低了内存需求并提升了吞吐性能，为资源受限设备上部署大型语言模型提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [28] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 矩阵值连续思维链模型中，潜在矩阵Z的秩无法追踪并行推理路径；秩截断不影响准确率，表明秩盲性源于位置无关性而非特定读出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究矩阵值连续思维链模型中，潜在矩阵Z的秩是否能作为结构可观测指标追踪并行推理路径，以及低秩截断是否会影响需要多组件推理的任务性能。

Method: 在四个训练设置（三个ProsQA，一个GSM8K-Aug）上测试秩-k投影消融曲线；设计四种读出机制（双线性重参数化、双线性加GELU、SVD增强、二次型）；以GPT-2 SFT为负对照；使用线性探针评估Z的信息含量。

Result: 秩-k投影消融曲线在所有条件下均呈平坦状（变化不超过0.6个百分点）；三种子重复实验准确率81.0±2.0%，而Z的有效秩跨度{4, 12, 13}；四种读出机制的秩-k曲线均平坦（Spearman p值0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46）；Z上线性探针AUC 0.673低于原始隐藏状态0.846；GPT-2 SFT负对照同样呈现平坦秩-k曲线。

Conclusion: 潜在矩阵Z的秩与推理能力无关，损失函数不奖励特定秩；秩盲性并非源于特定的先展平后投影读出机制；秩-k消融将秩盲性与位置无关性混淆，无法有效区分二者。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [29] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用深度光流网络替代传统窗口匹配进行立体风场反演，并通过蒸馏技术实现单卫星模型生成高精度大气运动矢量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMVs依赖数值天气预报背景场估算高度，存在循环依赖、高度不准确、计算成本高和覆盖率稀疏等问题。立体风场方法虽能消除NWP依赖并提高精度，但计算负担重且覆盖有限。

Method: 1. 采用深度光流网络替代窗口匹配进行立体匹配，平衡自监督几何残差损失与有监督探空重建进行微调；2. 通过知识蒸馏将立体教师模型压缩为单卫星学生模型，消除多卫星重叠要求；3. 学生模型模拟教师模型的卡方和高度不确定性以进行质量保证。

Result: 三重配准验证表明，立体风场方法在水汽波段（6.2、6.9、7.3 μm）性能优于业务AMVs，但在长波红外波段（11.2 μm）出现退化。学生模型能够实现全圆盘GEO影像的风场反演。

Conclusion: 基于深度光流的立体风场方法在水汽波段显著提升大气运动矢量精度，单卫星蒸馏模型实现了全球覆盖的高效风场反演，为改进数值天气预报数据同化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [30] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度学习中的扩展规律是否适用于精算费率厘定领域。研究发现，不同模型架构的数据扩展能力差异显著：TabM模型展现出比纯监督表格Transformer和MLP更强的数据扩展性；Transformer除非引入额外归纳偏置或自监督，否则参数扩展效果有限。这为精算任务中的模型选择提供了定量指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的扩展规律描述了模型性能随容量、数据和计算资源增加而提升的现象，通常呈幂律趋势。然而，在精算费率厘定领域，数据具有表格化、异质性和噪声大的特点，经典模型（如GLM）仍是强有力的基线。本文旨在探究类似的扩展规律是否存在于这一特殊领域，以及不同架构的扩展特性如何。

Method: 使用真实的机动车保险组合数据集，对来自不同模型家族的模型进行训练。通过增加训练数据比例和多次随机种子实验，评估样本外泊松偏差，这是一种基于似然的泊松计数预测损失指标，值越低表示样本外拟合越好。系统性地比较了TabM、纯监督表格Transformer、标准MLP等架构的扩展行为。

Result: 所有模型家族都随数据量增加而改进，但扩展指数存在显著差异：(1) TabM展现出比纯监督表格Transformer和标准MLP基线明显更强的数据扩展能力；(2) Transformer变体显示出较弱的参数扩展性，除非引入额外归纳偏置（如TabM风格适配）或自监督机制；(3) 简单增加Transformer规模所提供的增益有限。

Conclusion: 研究结果为不同数据规模下的模型选择提供了定量指导，表明精算表格任务中的有效扩展依赖于架构设计和损失函数目标设计，而非简单地增加模型规模。这强调了针对特定领域特性选择合适模型架构的重要性。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [31] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法（NTK-KIP、MetaQuill和MetaQuill-KIP），将神经切线核（NTK）回归与元学习结合，实现神经场在稀疏观测下的快速高质量非线性重建，避免依赖大型预训练生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场在稀疏观测下的快速高质量重建仍具挑战。经典NTK回归虽可闭式求解，但本质上是线性的，无法积累可复用的任务先验。本文旨在弥合解析核方法与实际少样本重建之间的差距。

Method: 提出三种算法：1) NTK-KIP学习蒸馏支持集，实现非线性表示和大规模缺失区域修复；2) MetaQuill元学习共享初始化，仅更新小任务偏移实现特征学习；3) MetaQuill-KIP融合两者，用KIP式非线性热启动初始化，再对元学习初始化进行小偏移精调。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下实现高PSNR重建和语义合理的修复，仅需轻量级单实例适应，相比依赖大型预训练生成先验和昂贵逐图像调参的扩散方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NTK驱动的神经场可以兼具非线性和元学习能力，有效弥合了解析核方法与实际少样本重建之间的差距，为神经场重建提供了一种高效的新范式。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [32] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示MoE+LoRA多域微调中路由分离无法避免负迁移，提出SpawnLoRA通过在专家内动态添加门控子适配器解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域微调中普遍假设MoE路由的token级分离能够隔离不同领域的参数更新，避免负迁移。然而这一假设缺乏实证验证，研究者旨在探究路由分离是否真正有效防止不同领域间的干扰。

Method: 在Python代码、生物医学文本和数学推理三个领域进行实验验证；提出Jaccard路由重叠度和适配器梯度余弦相似度两个诊断指标；设计SpawnLoRA方法，在检测到适配器级冲突时动态添加门控子适配器，同时保持路由器固定。

Result: 实验发现即使领域间专家路由近乎不重叠，添加生物医学数据仍显著增加代码困惑度；诊断显示干扰主要源于近似正交的领域梯度在低秩适配器子空间内竞争；SpawnLoRA在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B上有效降低了负迁移，优于标准LoRA和秩自适应LoRA。

Conclusion: MoE路由分离单独无法防止多域微调中的负迁移；专家内部的结构性分离比单纯的路由分离或秩扩展能提供更好的领域隔离效果；SpawnLoRA为多域微调提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [33] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型作为批量优化器的性能，发现在数值函数优化中LLMs表现有竞争力但脆弱，而在语义丰富的离散空间优化中表现显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型因其大规模预训练能够适应各种优化设置而成为有吸引力的优化先验，但现代推理LLMs在批量优化设置中的有效性仍缺乏探索，因此需要系统评估LLMs作为批量优化器的实际性能。

Method: 将当前一代前沿LLMs作为批量优化器，在连续和离散两种设置中进行系统性能评估，并与经典非LLM优化方法进行对比分析。

Result: LLMs作为数值测试函数的零样本批量优化器具有竞争力，但相比传统非LLM优化方法表现脆弱；在语义丰富的设置中，LLMs的先验知识表现显著更好，在结构与其预训练数据相似的离散空间中导航和推理时批量优化行为非常有效。

Conclusion: LLMs的批量优化能力在不同设置中表现差异显著：在传统数值优化中不如经典方法稳健，但在语义丰富的离散空间优化中展现出独特优势，表明LLMs的优化行为与其预训练数据结构密切相关。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [34] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将DECAF公平性机制迁移到9种生成器（边际分布、GAN、扩散模型及其差分隐私变体），在2,520次实验中验证了机制的普适性，新因果扩散骨干模型实现了最佳公平性，且公平性约束对数据保真度影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前DECAF方法仅在单一非隐私GAN上验证了通过因果图边切割实现公平性的机制，尚不清楚该机制是否属于DECAF特有或源于因果分解本身，需要系统评估其跨不同生成器家族的普适性。

Method: 将三种公平性定义转化为生成器因果图上的边切割操作，迁移至三个不同家族（边际分布、GAN、扩散模型）的九种生成器，包括差分隐私变体；在Adult和COMPAS数据集上进行2,520组配对实验，测试三个隐私保证等级。

Result: 公平性机制在所有生成器上均成功迁移；新提出的因果扩散骨干模型产生最公平的数据发布，保真度接近边际分布层级；边切割对保真度影响极小，仅使下游分类器AUC平均下降0.07-0.15；添加隐私保证不会降低数据公平性。

Conclusion: 基于因果图边切割的公平性机制具有跨生成器架构的普适性，不依赖于特定GAN结构；因果扩散模型在公平性与保真度权衡上表现最优，为敏感数据发布提供了可靠的公平性保障方案。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [35] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BioGlyph方法，将网络拓扑编译为结构角色的可解释语言表示，显著提升大语言模型在20个网络上的结构推理能力（准确率提升高达26个百分点），并在酵母蛋白质网络中发现生物学组织规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言处理方面表现出色，但在处理网络结构问题时面临困难。当网络以边列表、句子或测量表的形式呈现时，模型难以推断其结构含义，无法有效回答关于元素中心性、社区桥接连接以及元素移除后网络变化等结构推理问题。

Method: BioGlyph结合图划分和结构测量来识别网络中的关键角色（如枢纽节点、社区核心和跨社区连接器），并通过固定规则将这些角色转换为通用词汇。该方法为每个元素提供结构角色描述、支持证据和语义后果，同时保持网络和大语言模型不变。

Result: 在涵盖5个领域的20个网络上，BioGlyph显著提升了开放大语言模型的结构推理能力，在系统准确率上比基于边、数值和学习的表示方法高出最多26个百分点。消融实验表明性能提升源于用语义可解释术语显式编码结构角色。在密集、具有社区结构的网络中提升更为显著，而在稀疏网络中提升减少。在酵母蛋白质相互作用网络中，跨社区连接器富含必需基因，而外围蛋白质则较少。

Conclusion: BioGlyph为语言模型和科学家提供了可解释的网络结构推理表示方法，不仅提升了模型的结构推理性能，还能揭示生物学组织规律，证明了将结构信息编码为语义可解释术语的有效性。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged.
