Method: 提出固定地标点、每运动员-会话一个表示的建模框架。在地标点（如第10、20、30分钟）使用截止该时刻观测到的信息构建一个表示，保持目标标签在会话级别，避免不支持的分钟级损伤监督。评估了会话前、累积、动态和组合四种表示方式，采用运动员分离验证、运动员聚类Bootstrap不确定性分析等多种验证策略。

Result: 基于2020年SoccerMon数据集（48名精英女子足球运动员，3743个运动员-会话，其中22个损伤相关会话），主要结果为：累积+动态逻辑回归在各地标点获得ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607，PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150，且不确定性较大。包含会话前信息的表示在多个地标点显示出较高的点估计值，但不确定性仍然较高。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了监测数据时间粒度不匹配的建模问题，保持了标签在会话级别的合理性。然而，由于损伤样本稀少（仅22个损伤相关会话），模型性能有限且不确定性较大，未来需要更多数据来提升预测可靠性。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [72] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究有序伯努利字核函数及其反整数水平集几何，在复解析延拓下构建了与黎曼ζ函数临界零点相关的垂直几何结构，并提出了精确的整数量子化分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究伯努利字核函数$f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^{n-k}$的几何结构，探索其在二进制水平$2^{-n}$下的特殊性质，以及通过互补保持复延拓产生的共轭对称垂直几何与黎曼ζ函数临界线零点之间的联系。

Method: 采用有序伯努利字核函数及其反整数水平集；引入互补保持复延拓得到共轭对称垂直几何；利用二次坐标$Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2$进行精确整数量子化；对临界线零点纵坐标$\gamma_k$诱导的水平进行精确分解$L_k=N_k+\delta_k$；通过圆化$Z_k=\exp(2\pi i\delta_k)$分离残差相位；利用唯一分解将整数壳解析为素数生成器坐标；引入复指数$s$将构造提升到Dirichlet原子$m^{-s}$。

Result: 获得精确的整数量子化分解公式$L_k=1/4+\gamma_k^2=N_k+\delta_k$；残差$\delta_k$具有周期性第一伯努利残差性质；圆化后$Z_k=\exp(2\pi i\delta_k)$将$\gamma_k^2$模1隔离为残差相位变量；建立了Dirichlet级数与Euler乘积组装之间的联系。

Conclusion: 论文建立了伯努利核函数几何与黎曼ζ函数临界零点之间的精确数学结构关系，保持了精确恒等式、经典ζ函数连接、数值控制和开放条件Weyl检验的显式分离。作者明确声明未声称证明黎曼猜想。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [73] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种免费的暂停标记机制，在不增加推理上下文长度、KV缓存和延迟的前提下，通过并行预测流提升语言模型的下一个标记预测性能，在1B参数模型上提升2-3 centinats，训练开销仅增加1.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过在序列中添加暂停或思考标记来增加计算量以提升预测质量，但这会增加上下文长度、KV缓存和推理延迟。研究动机是寻找一种既能增加推理计算量又不在推理阶段产生额外成本的方法。

Method: 提出一种"免费暂停标记"机制，该标记在现有位置上通过并行预测流共享权重的主干网络进行额外计算，而不是作为序列中的额外标记。这种方法在推理时不增加上下文长度、KV缓存，且延迟增长可忽略不计。

Result: 在1B参数模型上，下一个标记预测性能提升2-3 centinats；训练开销相较于优化的预训练流程仅增加约1.14倍；推理时的额外浮点运算通常不影响吞吐量，因为其并非吞吐量的活跃瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持相同的计算量、参数量和标记数量的条件下，实现了对标准下一个标记训练Transformer模型的性能改进，为语言模型提供了在推理阶段"免费"获得额外计算量的新范式。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [74] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对等比较的无监督方法，通过引入能量感知的最小失真嵌入(MDE)技术，识别移动网络站点中未被发现的能效问题，帮助运营商节省运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商的能耗是最大的运营支出之一，但由于缺乏真实的能效问题标签且历史测量数据中可能已经包含固有的低效问题，站点级别的能效问题（如故障冷却控制器、空闲无线电设备和寄生辅助负载）往往无法被检测到。

Method: 提出了一种无监督的对等比较方法，基于具有相似结构和运行特征的站点应表现出可比能耗的前提。引入了一种新颖的能量感知最小失真嵌入(MDE)公式，通过基于能量的排斥机制扩展标准MDE目标，鼓励相对于可比对等站点具有异常高能耗的站点在嵌入空间中偏离其局部邻域，生成同时保留结构相似性并编码能量相关偏差的低维表示。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法优于传统的异常检测基线方法，能够有效识别潜在的低效站点，为大规模移动网络能效优化提供了可靠基础。

Conclusion: 该方法通过派生的异常分数为优先安排现场调查提供了实用机制，使移动网络运营商能够将工程资源集中在最有可能产生节能效益的站点上，从而实现大规模的能源效率优化。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [75] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WAND，一种原生可解释的无监督表格异常检测器，在保持准确性的同时提供无需额外计算的特征级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督异常检测方法通常只提供异常分数而无法解释为何某个点被标记为异常。当前的主流解释方法（如SHAP或LIME）是事后附加的，需要对检测器进行数千次重新查询，仅能近似解释，且计算成本高昂。

Method: WAND围绕单位球面上的方向组织计算，通过投影偏离子高斯极值基线的程度来评分。关键创新在于：标记异常点的见证方向本身就是其解释（特征空间中的向量），在评分过程中零成本获得特征归因；由于评分可微分，可通过梯度恢复解释。该方法的评分复杂度与样本量呈线性关系。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND与16个无监督基线方法达到ROC-AUC持平的最佳平均Friedman排名，证明了在无精度损失的前提下获得可解释性；其原生解释比事后SHAP/LIME和ECOD更准确、更忠实，且查询成本仅为后者的极小部分。

Conclusion: WAND是一种实用且可解释的异常检测解决方案，实现了在无准确性代价的情况下获得可解释性，为可解释异常检测提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多步近端策略改进（MPI）框架，通过几何视角将离线强化学习演员更新解释为流形梯度流，实现可控的策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临两个竞争需求：策略更新需保持在数据集支持的动作附近以保持价值估计可靠，而显著的性能提升往往需要超越行为分布。现有方法难以平衡这两者。

Method: 将策略建模为具有度量几何的概率流形，将广泛的离线演员目标解释为由评论家定义能量诱导的流形梯度流的隐式离散化。提出多步近端策略改进（MPI），通过组合连续的重新中心近端步骤，实现受控的策略改进。框架适用于确定性策略和对角高斯策略。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，少量MPI改进步骤提升了强离线基线方法（TD3+BC、ReBRAC、IQL）的性能。诊断实验区分了重新中心改进与固定目标更新调度，并刻画了评论家误差下的局限性。

Conclusion: MPI提供了一个通用、灵活的插件式改进机制，在保持近端控制的同时实现超越数据集支持的可控策略改进，为离线强化学习提供了新的几何视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [77] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XCal-FL，一种闭环、可解释性驱动的联邦学习本地训练算法，通过预测logit变化、反事实边界和显著性集中度三个信号动态校准差分隐私噪声，在保证隐私的前提下显著提升模型准确性和解释保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要可信解释的场景（如辅助临床诊断）中，差分隐私噪声会扭曲学习到的表示并降低解释保真度。现有工作采用静态特征重要性信号来适应DP噪声，这限制了可解释性只能进行事后分析，无法在训练过程中根据解释质量校准噪声。

Method: 提出XCal-FL算法，一种闭环、可解释性驱动的跨数据孤岛联邦学习本地训练方法，通过三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声：(1)预测logit变化—衡量对模型置信度的因果影响；(2)反事实边界—捕捉决策边界敏感性；(3)显著性集中度—量化模型注意力的空间一致性，同时通过自适应隐私核算保证形式化的DP保证。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集和不同FL配置下的实验表明，XCal-FL产生更准确和可解释的全局模型，相比静态噪声FL预测性能提升超过10%，解释保真度提升高达5倍，在保真度方面优于最先进的自适应DP方法。XCal-FL还实现了更高的隐私预算效率，使每单位累积隐私损失转化为更大的准确性和解释保真度增益。分析进一步揭示了可解释性是隐私权衡的一个独立维度，呈现非线性动态，无法仅从效用推断。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可解释性是隐私权衡中一个独特的维度，与预测性能（近似线性关系）不同，解释保真度表现出非线性动态特征，不能仅从效用推断，这对决策关键应用的训练和隐私预算分配具有重要意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [78] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维极限下分析了注意力机制的训练动态，揭示了不同的注意力参数化方式（直接优化、绑定注意力、非绑定注意力）会产生不同的隐式偏置和收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心组件，但其训练动态仍缺乏深入理解，特别是当注意力矩阵具有较高秩时。现有研究对注意力机制如何学习以及不同参数化方式的影响认识不足，亟需一个理论框架来分析多层多头注意力架构的训练过程。

Method: 作者提出了注意力索引模型框架，该框架可表示多层多头注意力架构。在高维极限下，证明了总体损失景观可由有限个迹序参量刻画，而在线随机梯度下降由矩阵矩的无穷层次控制，该层次可被有限截断系统指数级近似。通过比较三种参数化方式：直接优化注意力矩阵S、绑定注意力（S=WW^T）和非绑定注意力（S=UV^T），分析其训练动态和对称性破缺机制。

Result: 1）直接优化注意力矩阵S容易陷入无信息状态；2）绑定注意力（S=WW^T）具有自动对称性破缺机制，在Θ(d²log d)样本量下实现弱恢复；3）非绑定注意力（S=UV^T）展现快慢机制：预激活均值在快时间尺度演化，重叠矩阵在慢时间尺度演化，当快动力学选择的状态打破初始对称性时，在Θ(d²log d)尺度实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化本身可作为一种架构级隐式偏置发挥作用。不同参数化方式决定了模型能否有效打破对称性并从数据中学习有用信息。绑定和非绑定注意力机制都提供了打破对称性的途径，而直接优化则缺乏这种机制。这为理解和设计更有效的注意力架构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [79] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIFFINT提出了一种可解释的自编码器异常检测方法，通过学习软区间隶属度实现特征级别的异常解释，在保持检测精度的同时提供可审计的约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重建的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确但缺乏可解释性，无法告诉实践者哪些特征范围导致样本异常。研究者希望构建一个既能保持可微表示学习能力，又能暴露可检查内部结构的异常检测模型。

Method: DIFFINT将自编码器的潜在瓶颈结构化为软的、轴对齐的区间隶属度集合，端到端地从原始数值数据中学习（无需离散化或二值化）。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中人类可读的超矩形；实例通过其相对于其他单元落入每个区间的强度进行编码，重建误差作为异常分数。模型采用Lipschitz约束的解码器，并为落在学习支持区域外每个活动坐标之外的点提供认证的重建误差下界。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准测试、22个基线方法、统一[-1,1]归一化协议下，DIFFINT在ROC-AUC（4.10）和AUPR（4.16）上均获得最佳平均排名。在仅内点检测器中明显领先，与最强的污染数据检测器具有竞争力。它是统计上并列领先的七种方法中唯一可解释的检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT成功地在异常检测精度与可解释性之间取得了平衡，通过封闭式、无标签的重要性排名将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束，无需看到异常标签即可实现特征级别的异常解释，为需要可审计性的应用场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 持续知识更新方法的性能排名会随评估时间点和基线方法的适配容量而变化，仅凭单一检查点和固定适配器秩进行比较会产生误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅从单一最终检查点和传统适配器秩来宣称某种持续知识更新方法的优越性，作者发现这种评估方式可能不足以识别更优的操作点。

Method: 在固定的周期层级方法与累积回放基线之间进行24个月的Wikidata流对比实验，系统地变化评估月份、回放LoRA秩和查询表述，以探索方法排名的稳定性。

Result: 方法排名在不同评估区域会发生逆转：在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，层级方法相比秩-8回放的5.0点优势变为相对于秩-72回放的11.6点劣势；Llama-3.2-1B和保留释义数据上观察到相同的秩条件性逆转现象。

Conclusion: 提出新的评估协议：报告性能轨迹和容量扫描，仅当方法排序在整个评估区域稳定时才声明鲁棒的优胜者，否则应报告优胜者区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在此协议下，周期层级方法是一个更新成本较低的操作点，而非质量上的优胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [81] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RATL方法，通过检索历史预测残差来修正冻结的基础预测器，在多元时间序列预测中实现了插拔式的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索增强生成（RAG）方法直接复用检索到的目标值在回归任务中不够鲁棒，因为样本在输出水平、数值尺度和局部动态上存在差异。此外，传统预测流程虽然使用残差进行模型优化，但未将历史残差样本作为推理时可访问的记忆保留。

Method: RATL冻结基础预测器构建检索键，将历史预测残差转化为该基础模型专用的训练记忆。推理时，在满足因果可用性约束下检索相似历史上下文的残差轨迹，通过基于预测块和变量的集合感知路由器选择并组合这些轨迹，实现反馈修正。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息。RATL在大多数实验设置中改进了冻结的基础预测器。在真实基准测试中以iTransformer为主要基础预测器，RATL在多数设置下进一步提升了基础预测器的性能，并展现出跨骨干网络的迁移能力。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为基础模型特定的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供了一种基于残差记忆的学习反馈修正的新范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [82] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay方法，通过Headroom（剩余学习价值）和Drift（与当前策略兼容性）两个决策机制选择重放轨迹，在数学推理、多模态推理和智能体搜索基准上超越朴素重放并匹配或超越更复杂的重放方法，在智能体搜索中显著降低实际运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的推理模型后训练中，重复生成新的轨迹（rollout）成为性能瓶颈，尤其在智能体环境中环境交互主导了实际运行成本。现有重放方法将其嵌入包含探索、经验重构或混合策略优化的复杂训练流程中，难以分离重放本身的贡献。研究聚焦于：仅依靠有原则的重放选择能走多远？

Method: 为GRPO引入Headroom-Drift Replay群体级重放控制原语：Headroom根据剩余学习价值对存储的群体进行排序，Drift通过与当前策略的兼容性对其进行门控。保持新鲜在线策略流不变，不添加任何辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和智能体搜索基准上，单一干预方法优于朴素重放，在Avg Mean@32指标上匹配或超越更广泛的重放方法。在环境交互主导成本的智能体搜索中，在显著降低实际运行时间的同时达到相当的质量。

Conclusion: 有原则的重放选择机制本身即可取得显著效果，无需复杂的辅助训练管道。Headroom-Drift Replay通过学习价值和策略兼容性的双维度决策，有效缓解了强化学习后训练中的轨迹生成瓶颈。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [83] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV提出了一种针对NoPE MLA模型的长KV cache压缩方法，利用模型自带的64维解耦分支作为查询无关的重要性信号，在无需训练、量化和权重修改的情况下，实现了高达128倍的压缩比且检索性能不下降。该方法在RoPE MLA上完全失效，证明了其NoPE排他性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于观测注意力选择（如H2O、SnapKV）的KV cache压缩方法在长生命周期场景下失效，因为当需要压缩时，查询尚未存在，token的重要性还未被观测到，导致NoPE MLA模型上的检索性能崩溃至0.00-0.33。作者需要找到一种查询无关的重要性信号来解决这个问题。

Method: VestigeKV利用NoPE训练后重新利用的64维解耦分支（RoPE遗留物）作为salience通道，按查询无关信号进行驱逐决策。读取每行11%的数据，将top-m行保留在attended tier，其他所有行不删除地移动到GPU驻留的archive中（可通过触发器访问）。无需训练、量化和任何权重或内核修改。

Result: 从8k到65k上下文范围内，8倍压缩下检索保持1.00，32倍压缩下达到0.92，与完整行选择无差距。recall tier配置下128倍压缩仍保持1.00。Attended tier仅占每token cache的0.25 KB（在32倍压缩时）。Archive保持位精确且GPU驻留，支持主机卸载以回收VRAM。在RoPE MLA上性能崩溃至0.08，证明了方法的NoPE排他性。

Conclusion: 该方法仅适用于NoPE MLA模型，在RoPE MLA上完全失效。作者假设Kimi K3（NoPE Gated-MLA变体）可能适用，但仅声明对实测模型有效。论文提供了冻结的阈值、20个归档裁决和8条封闭路径，确保可复现性。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [84] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于统计重分布的输出空间标签删除方法，无需访问原始数据或调整特征空间，在计算效率和隐私保护方面显著优于现有的重训练和特征空间调整方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签删除方法（重训练和特征空间调整）存在依赖原始数据、计算存储成本高、结果不一致、可扩展性差、模型效用退化等局限，亟需一种低成本、高隐私、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种统计重分布方法，通过在输出空间近似重训练模型的后删除置信度向量，实现模块化的输出过滤器，仅依赖现有标签和先验输出置信度，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛。

Result: 在多个分类任务上，该方法在性能上与完整重训练相当，同时显著提升计算效率和隐私保护能力。

Conclusion: 该统计重分布输出空间方法为分类系统中的标签删除问题提供了一种高效、隐私友好、可扩展的解决方案，有效克服了现有方法的主要局限。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [85] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RobustSeiz框架，这是一个开源、模型无关的癫痫检测器压力测试基准平台，用于在真实临床部署前评估检测器在分布偏移、噪声和对抗性输入下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管癫痫检测器在留出的脑电图(EEG)数据上表现良好，但在真实世界的数据采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入条件下可能失效。现有评估方法缺乏在临床相关的分布偏移下进行标准化压力测试和比较的协议。

Method: 1) 将四个公开头皮EEG数据集(CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, SeizeIT1)标准化为BIDS-EEG格式；2) 实现环境、噪声和对抗性变换的超参数网格搜索；3) 提供样本级和事件级评估指标（敏感性、精确率、F1、24小时假阳性率、发作时间偏差、MC dropout预测一致性）；4) 开发Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册中心及完整评估和研究子集两种模式。

Result: 在TUSZ数据集上使用当代癫痫检测器演示框架效果，完成了完整的变换网格评估；通过加性高斯白噪声(AWGN)分析展示了扰动强度如何影响检测质量、发作时间定位和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为评估癫痫检测器在真实临床压力条件下的鲁棒性提供了共享基准标准，将部署前评估从清洁数据准确率扩展到更全面的鲁棒性验证。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Uno模型，通过将LLM参数解耦为AR权重和轻量级扩散权重，实现自回归模型分布下的多token并行生成，无需额外草稿模型即可获得高达3倍的无损加速，且性能优于现有扩散LLM和推测解码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的自回归结构导致token生成必须顺序进行，造成推理速度瓶颈。传统解决方案如推测解码需要额外的草稿模型，而扩散LLM会牺牲自回归模型的质量。作者希望在不损害模型质量的前提下，实现并行token生成以加速推理。

Method: 将模型参数解耦为两组：使用标准NTP目标训练的AR权重，以及轻量级扩散权重（通过Diffusion Distillation阶段学习，几乎不增加训练开销）。引入Ψ-Spec采样器家族，实现固定上下文长度下的无损加速和推理时扩展。模型可从头训练或通过增强现有开源AR LLM构建。

Result: Uno在所有评估批次大小下均优于领先的推测解码方法的吞吐量，相对基础AR模型实现高达3倍加速（包括设备支持的最大批次大小）。8B参数的Uno模型在智能体工具使用、编程和长上下文推理等基准测试中均优于26B的DiffusionGemma和专有的Mercury 2。

Conclusion: 扩散增强LLM成功将扩散过程融入自回归框架，实现了并行token生成与模型质量保持的统一，为LLM推理加速提供了无需草稿模型的新范式，在多种任务和规模下均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [87] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对处理组极端分位数效应估计中存在的位置非不变性问题，提出了基于倾向得分加权的因果极值指数估计器和差分外推方案，实现了位置不变性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极端分位数处理效应估计器在潜在结果分布存在位置平移时缺乏不变性，尽管总体QTE本身具有位置不变性。这一缺陷在重尾分布和极端分位数估计中尤为关键，亟需开发满足位置不变性的估计方法。

Method: 分两步解决：首先，利用逆倾向得分加权方法将位置不变的Fraga极值指数估计器适配到因果推断框架；其次，采用差分外推方案替代传统外推公式，在分位数差分计算中消除位置参数的影响。

Result: 证明了所提极端分位数处理效应估计器的一致性和渐近正态性，构建了方差的一致估计器，实现了渐近有效的统计推断。模拟研究验证了位置不变性、阈值选择的稳定性以及置信区间的覆盖率表现。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了极端分位数处理效应估计中的位置不变性问题，为重尾分布场景下的因果推断提供了理论可靠、实践稳定的估计工具。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [88] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PreferenceEKF方法，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波在低维参数空间中追踪奖励模型不确定性，解决了RLHF中主动学习的样本效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习人类反馈(RLHF)虽然强大但样本效率低下，主动学习需要有效的不确定性量化。然而大规模神经网络奖励模型的不确定性量化仍是一个关键挑战，传统方法在全参数空间进行后验推断计算代价过高。

Method: 将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间中采用扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)进行序贯推断，随新偏好查询到来持续更新奖励模型后验，从而实现神经网络参数的可扩展采样以高效计算获取函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准测试上，相比其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面表现更优；学习到的奖励模型在离线强化学习策略性能上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 验证了可扩展贝叶斯方法在基于偏好的奖励建模中的潜力，为RLHF框架下的高效主动学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [89] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于因果最优传输的近似损失函数，用于解决奇异扩散系数和条件密度不光滑情况下的条件生成模型训练问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型在训练过程中严重依赖分数函数进行引导，但当生成模型为具有奇异扩散系数的扩散过程，且底层条件密度不存在或不够平滑时，传统的分数引导方法面临挑战。

Method: 利用因果最优传输理论及其通过条件扩散过程的可预测表示性质进行刻画，结合Üstünel提出的良性定义鞅问题框架，定义近似损失函数来识别最小熵控制。

Result: 在最小假设条件下，成功识别出用于引导的最小熵控制方法，为奇异扩散系数和密度不光滑情况下的条件生成提供了理论解决方案。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为条件生成模型提供了一种在最小假设下的有效理论框架，能够处理传统分数引导方法难以解决的奇异扩散系数和密度不光滑问题。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [90] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Direct-P方法和因果路径，成功利用Blackwell的FP4张量核心加速注意力计算，前向传播吞吐量最高提升2.13倍，完整训练更新加速1.14倍，同时解决了MXFP4训练轨迹发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell架构的4位浮点(FP4)张量核心本应加速注意力计算，但由于矩阵乘法规模缩小后，softmax转换和片上依赖成为性能瓶颈，导致无法自动获得加速效果。

Method: 针对非因果推理提出Direct-P方法，将分数直接映射到FP4概率；针对因果路径，将前向量化直接传递到反向传播，从保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数。

Result: Direct-P在NVIDIA GB200上达到BF16前向吞吐量的2.13倍；因果路径使完整的单GPU 80亿参数更新加速1.14倍；分布式训练中FP8概率和数值保持稳定，而MXFP4概率/数值的训练轨迹全部发散。

Conclusion: 成功解决了FP4注意力加速的技术障碍，证明了在保持数值稳定性前提下，低精度格式可以显著提升大规模模型训练和推理效率，但需要注意MXFP4格式存在训练不稳定问题。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [91] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HOOD的无耦合学习算法，在任意N玩家正常形式博弈中实现了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾上界，突破了以往方法在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾的关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多玩家博弈中，现有方法难以在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾，主要障碍是博弈过程中的大幅振荡问题。研究者希望设计一种算法能够控制并抑制这种振荡，从而突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出HOOD算法（高阶乐观主义与折扣），这是乐观跟随正则化领导者(OptFTRL)的变体。算法结合了折扣(N+1)阶预测器和熵正则化，在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"上进行操作。这种组合设计专门用于以可控方式抑制博弈序列的大幅振荡。

Result: 在任意N玩家正常形式博弈中（每个玩家最多K个动作），HOOD算法保证了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾上界，该界限在博弈时长范围内均匀成立。该算法与同期独立工作（Liu等人，遗憾上界O(N²¹log⁴K)）存在显著相似性。

Conclusion: 通过折扣高阶预测器与熵正则化的创新组合，HOOD算法成功解决了先前尝试在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾的关键障碍，为多玩家博弈的无耦合学习提供了更优的理论保障。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [92] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs）和前瞻性输入编码，有效解决了深度连续时间循环网络中信号延迟和梯度衰减问题，在语音识别和长序列任务上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间循环网络通过时间积分获得记忆，但在深度堆叠中会导致自下而上信号延迟和自上而下误差衰减。这种失败模式限制了深层循环网络的性能，需要找到解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs）：生物启发的复值时间滤波器，是对角状态空间模型（SSMs）的特例。从能量模型推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。2. 引入前瞻性输入编码：使用无参数双抽头更新使每层的自下而上输入具有前瞻性，保持循环转移和并行扫描不变。3. 将此校正扩展到一般对角SSMs，证明能缓解时序梯度截断（即仅空间反向传播）时的深度依赖梯度衰减。

Result: 1. 在全BPTT训练下，前瞻性变体在所有模型和配置中匹配或超越非前瞻性对照。2. 非残差宽度32的6层RQF在原始音频Speech Commands上达到96.09%准确率，参数量31.9k。3. 宽度64的6层RQF在16384步Path-X任务上达到83.56%。4. 确认RQF是参数高效的循环基础结构，前瞻性输入编码是深度连续时间循环网络的输入侧校正方法。

Conclusion: RQFs作为参数高效的循环基础结构，配合前瞻性输入编码，成功解决了深度连续时间循环网络中因时间积分导致的信号延迟和梯度衰减问题，为深层时序模型的训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [93] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个低成本、开源的微型阿克曼车辆端到端自动驾驶实验平台，包含物理车辆、城市赛道、数字孪生等组件，并实现了命令条件行为克隆基线。实验表明，真实环境平均横向误差6.1cm（接近人类演示的4.7cm），数字孪生中FOV从58°扩大到120°可将误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。结合合成数据和真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一完成所有四条赛道的配置，验证了平台作为仿真到现实研究测试床的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究缺乏低成本、可重复的实验平台，仿真方法与真实世界执行之间存在差距，需要建立能够连接仿真和现实世界的开源测试床来支持可重现的研究。

Method: 构建低成本开源实验平台，整合物理微型阿克曼车辆、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生。实现命令条件行为克隆基线方法：神经网络接收车载相机图像和高级导航命令，输出转向和速度。在数字孪生中生成合成驾驶数据，使用学习到的仿真到现实图像转换器减少外观差距，结合真实演示数据训练高容量策略。

Result: 真实闭环实验中，学习策略能够跟车道并执行转向命令，平均横向误差6.1cm，接近人类演示的4.7cm。数字孪生实验显示相机视场角对性能影响显著，FOV从58°扩大到120°可使平均横向误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。在四条赛道闭环测试中，仅结合合成数据和真实演示训练的高容量策略能够完成所有赛道，而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据训练的网络完成数量较少。

Conclusion: 该开源平台成功建立了实用的仿真到现实研究测试床，提供了初始的命令条件模仿学习基线。平台能够支持可控实验，连接基于仿真的自动驾驶方法与真实世界执行，为可重现研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>

### [94] [The Illusion of Independent Quorums: Epistemic Fault Domains and Correlated Cognitive Failures in Agentic Quorums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02925)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 多智能体仲裁机制虽然看似通过多方投票降低了风险，但由于共享上游依赖，当上游出现故障时会导致多个投票方同时受到影响，系统韧性被高估。本文引入了认识论故障域(EFDs)和结构认识论割κ_E来量化真实韧性，并设计了运行时控制器DAQC来强制执行这些结构性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高风险基础设施与策略变更依赖于多智能体仲裁授权，然而不同的审核者往往共享上游遥测数据、文档或工具后端。当上游输入发生故障时，多个投票会坍缩到单一被污染的根源上——复制并不意味着认识论上的冗余。这种隐藏的共享依赖导致系统韧性被误判。

Method: 引入认识论故障域(EFDs)和结构认识论割κ_E，用于量化相对于明确认识论故障基所需的最小建模根故障数，使其覆盖授权联盟。在闭合因果核算、保守暴露和授权对齐条件下，证明了κ_E下界于语义妥协所需根数κ_S。理论证明：(1)任意大仲裁可保持κ_E=1；(2)识别共享祖先永不增加可信韧性；(3)在兼容暴露扩展下，固定阈值增加投票者无法增加割值。同时设计了依赖感知仲裁控制器(DAQC)在运行时准入时强制执行结构性割。

Result: 理论证明：仲裁规模与真实认识论冗余解耦，大规模仲裁可能仍仅依赖单一故障源(κ_E=1)；识别共享依赖不会错误地提高韧性评估；简单增加投票者数量无法提升结构性割。DAQC控制器的机制通过分析推导和仿真验证，并提供了冻结的120任务外部基准测试套件。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了多智能体仲裁中共享依赖导致的隐性脆弱性，提供了量化真实韧性的理论框架(κ_E)和实际执行工具(DAQC)。对于高风险系统设计，必须显式建模认识论故障域，而非仅依赖表面的投票者数量来保证授权安全性。

Abstract: Multi-agent quorums are widely used to authorize high-stakes infrastructure and policy mutations, yet distinct reviewers often share upstream telemetry, documents, or tool backends. When upstream inputs fail, multiple votes collapse onto a single corrupted cause: replication does not imply epistemic redundancy. We introduce Epistemic Fault Domains (EFDs) and the Structural Epistemic Cut κ_E, which quantifies the minimum number of modeled root faults whose exposure covers an authorizing coalition relative to an explicit Epistemic Fault Basis. Under closed causal accounting, conservative exposure, and authorization alignment, κ_E lower-bounds the number of roots required for semantic compromise (κ_S). We prove that arbitrarily large quorums can retain κ_E=1, that recognizing shared ancestry never increases credited resilience, and that adding voters at a fixed threshold cannot increase the cut under compatible exposure extensions. Finally, we design the Dependency-Aware Quorum Controller (DAQC) to enforce structural cuts at runtime admission, evaluate its mechanics via analytical derivations and simulations, and provide a frozen 120-task external benchmark suite.

</details>


### [95] [Fully Fluctuating Sleepy Consensus from Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03063)
*Javier Nieto,Yuval Efron,Joachim Neu,Ling Ren*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个基于PKI和VRF最小假设的休眠共识协议，实现了完全波动的参与度和外部敌手模型下的安全性，无需VDF或硬件假设。关键创新是"分级唤醒"原语，使节点能对其他节点的活跃状态形成一致意见，并支持腐败节点恢复诚实状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币PoW协议具有高度鲁棒性，允许参与者自由休息并可恢复诚实状态，仅需诚实算力占多数。然而，基于PoS的休眠共识协议在匹配这种鲁棒性方面表现不佳，现有协议在标准PKI模型中必须严格限制敌手参与的波动。虽然近期工作通过外部敌手模型实现了完全波动参与，但依赖VDF等强密码学原语，这与PoW的简洁性相悖。

Method: 提出基于PKI和VRF的最小假设休眠共识协议：1）引入"分级唤醒"（graded wakeness）原语，使节点能对其他节点的活跃状态形成一致意见；2）在诚实多数假设下实现完全波动的参与度；3）扩展协议支持腐败节点恢复诚实状态（uncorruption），仅需对VRF输出的不可预测性做温和假设来保证活性。

Result: 成功构建了满足完全波动参与度的休眠共识协议：1）仅需PKI和VRF最小假设，无需VDF或硬件假设；2）在外部敌手模型和诚实多数条件下实现安全性；3）支持腐败节点转换为诚实节点；4）相比依赖VDF的方案，大幅降低了密码学假设强度。

Conclusion: 本研究弥合了PoS协议与比特币PoW协议在参与灵活性方面的差距，证明了在最小密码学假设下即可实现高度鲁棒的共识机制。分级唤醒原语为处理动态参与和节点状态转换提供了新思路，对设计更实用的区块链共识协议具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Bitcoin's proof-of-work (PoW)-based protocol is remarkable for how little it asks of its participants. Not only can miners take breaks from work whenever they please, but it is almost unique in offering a path of contrition: corrupt miners can reclaim honest status simply by resuming mining on the longest chain. The protocol only requires that honest miners hold the majority of computational power at any given time. Analogous proof-of-stake (PoS) protocols, usually formalized via the sleepy model of Pass and Shi (2017), have fallen short of matching this robustness. In fact, sleepy consensus protocols in the plain PKI model must heavily restrict fluctuations in adversarial participation over time. The recent work of Efron, Neu, and Pitassi (2025) enables fully fluctuating participation in the sleepy model by introducing the external adversary model. Their protocol, however, relies on verifiable delay functions (VDFs), a strong cryptographic primitive that somewhat resembles PoW, by assuming that the adversary cannot compute sequential work significantly faster than honest nodes.
  In this work, we design a sleepy consensus protocol for fully fluctuating participation with an external adversary under an honest majority, from minimal assumptions: a public key infrastructure (PKI) and a verifiable random function (VRF). In particular, we make no VDF or hardware assumptions. Our key technique is graded wakeness, a novel primitive that allows nodes to form consistent opinions on which other nodes are awake. We further extend our protocol to handle uncorruption, where corrupt nodes return to honesty. This extension requires only a mild additional assumption on the unpredictability of VRF outputs for liveness.

</details>


### [96] [Skywing: A Platform for Decentralized Mathematical Computing in Unreliable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03145)
*Alyson Fox,Colin Ponce,Annika Mauro,Wayne Mitchell,Sarah Osborn,Tom Benson,Shayna Kapadia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Skywing是一个开源平台，用于在不可靠环境中进行去中心化数学计算，通过代理、处理器和迭代三个抽象层次，实现了数学逻辑与通信基础设施的分离，支持多种去中心化算法的部署与研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的边缘计算、自主系统和信息物理系统需要在通过不可靠通信网络连接的异构设备上进行数学计算。传统高性能计算和分布式数据处理框架适用于托管环境，但在无法保证中心化协调、可靠通信和全局同步的去中心化环境中表现不佳，亟需一种能够在不可靠环境下进行去中心化数学计算的解决方案。

Method: 提出Skywing开源平台，采用三层抽象编程模型：代理表示去中心化计算中的参与者，处理器封装算法特定的更新规则，迭代管理分布式执行。平台支持异步操作、发布-订阅通信、托管消息处理，以及将独立算法组合成复杂的去中心化工作流，实现了数学逻辑与通信和执行基础设施的分离。

Result: 使用共识、优化和数值线性代数领域的代表性算法进行了验证，包括Push Sum和Max Consensus、组合监控与控制工作流、延迟通信下的弹性Push Sum，以及恶意数据篡改下的弹性异步Jacobi算法。实验表明Skywing能够支持多样化的去中心化算法，成功将数学逻辑与通信和执行基础设施分离。

Conclusion: Skywing成功填补了去中心化数学计算平台的空白，既可作为去中心化应用的部署框架，也可作为开发弹性数学算法的研究平台，为不可靠环境下的去中心化计算提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging edge, autonomous, and cyber-physical systems increasingly require mathematical computation across heterogeneous devices connected by unreliable communication networks. Traditional high-performance computing and distributed data-processing frameworks provide powerful abstractions for managed environments but are less suited to decentralized settings where centralized coordination, reliable communication, and global synchronization cannot be assumed. This paper presents Skywing, an open-source platform for decentralized mathematical computing in unreliable environments. Its programming model consists of three abstractions: agents represent participants in a decentralized computation, processors encapsulate algorithm-specific update rules, and iterations manage distributed execution. Skywing supports asynchronous operation, publish-subscribe communication, managed message handling, and the composition of independent algorithms into complex decentralized workflows. We demonstrate Skywing using representative algorithms from consensus, optimization, and numerical linear algebra. Experiments on the native Skywing runtime include Push Sum and Max Consensus, a composed monitoring and control workflow, resilient Push Sum under delayed communication, and resilient asynchronous Jacobi under malevolent data corruption. These demonstrations show that Skywing supports diverse decentralized algorithms while separating mathematical logic from communication and execution infrastructure. Skywing serves as both a deployment framework for decentralized applications and a research platform for developing resilient mathematical algorithms.

</details>


### [97] [BASP: Communication-Efficient Batch-Aware Sequence Parallelism for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03151)
*Bigyan Ghimire,Jon C. Calhoun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出BASP（批次感知序列并行）方法，通过利用批次结构来优化大语言模型的长序列训练。该方法根据微批次大小将GPU划分为不相交的序列并行组，减少全对全通信组大小，从而局部化通信并提高训练效率。在NVIDIA A100集群上的实验表明，与标准序列并行基线相比，BASP在Llama和Qwen模型上将端到端训练时间提高了1.17-1.31倍，同时保持相同的模型精度和内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列训练在大语言模型中日益重要，但现有的序列并行方法存在批次无关性缺陷——对所有批次大小应用统一的序列划分，导致通信效率低下。这造成了大量内存和通信需求，成为训练瓶颈。

Method: 提出批次感知序列并行（BASP）方法，核心思想是利用批次结构来减少通信开销。具体做法是根据微批次大小将GPU划分为不相交的序列并行组，这种设计能够减少all-to-all通信组的规模，实现通信的局部化，从而提升训练效率。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100集群上进行了实验验证，结果显示：BASP相比标准序列并行基线，在Llama和Qwen模型上实现了1.17-1.31倍的端到端训练时间提升，同时完全保持了模型精度和内存使用的一致性。

Conclusion: BASP成功解决了现有序列并行方法中的通信效率问题，通过智能利用批次结构实现了显著的训练加速。该方法在不牺牲模型性能的前提下，有效降低了长序列训练的通信开销，为大语言模型的长上下文训练提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning for large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly important, but training over long sequences remains challenging due to massive memory and communication requirements. Sequence parallelism has emerged as an essential technique for addressing bottlenecks in long sequence LLM training. However, we observe that existing sequence parallelism methods are batch-agnostic and apply uniform sequence partitioning across all batch sizes, resulting in inefficient communication. In this paper, we introduce Batch- Aware Sequence Parallelism (BASP), a sequence parallelism approach that leverages batch structure to reduce communication overhead. BASP exploits batch structure by partitioning GPUs into disjoint sequence-parallel groups according to the micro- batch size. This design reduces the all-to-all communication group size, thereby localizing communication and improving training efficiency. Experimental results on an NVIDIA A100 cluster show that BASP improves end-to-end training time by up to 1.17 - 1.31x in Llama and Qwen models compared to standard sequence parallel baselines, while preserving identical model accuracy and memory usage.

</details>


### [98] [Carbon-aware Resource Management for Latency-Sensitive Cloud Computing Environments: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03270)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria Rodriguez Read,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个详细的分类法，用于分析延迟敏感云计算环境中的碳感知资源管理，识别了现有研究的差距并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着云延迟敏感工作负载的增长，云服务提供商持续扩展基础设施，导致碳足迹增加并挑战净零排放目标；由于延迟优化的面向性能的刚性部署模式，减少其碳足迹具有挑战性。

Method: 构建了一个关于延迟敏感云计算环境中碳感知资源管理的详细分类法，并基于该分类法分析现有文献，讨论其优化方面。

Result: 分析了现有研究中的优化方面，识别了研究差距，并强调了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够利用应用特定机会的高效技术来减少延迟敏感云工作负载的碳足迹。

Abstract: Proliferation of cloud-based latency-sensitive workloads requires infrastructures tuned to their workload-specific latency constraints. Today, they shape the cloud from a generalized computing platform to diverse workload-specific cloud environments. As the demand for latency-sensitive workloads increases, cloud service providers continue to scale their infrastructure, adversely increasing the carbon footprint and challenging climate-crisis-driven net-zero emission goals. Due to performance-oriented rigid deployment patterns of latency-optimizations, reducing its carbon footprint is challenging. Therefore, efficient techniques that exploit application specific opportunities are needed in that. To this end, we present a detailed taxonomy of recent literature on carbon-aware resource management in latency-sensitive cloud computing environments. Using the taxonomy, we analyze existing works discussing their optimization aspects, identify the gaps, and highlight future research directions.

</details>


### [99] [A Technique for Load Shifting Low-latency Applications in Multi-Region Renewables Harvesting via SMT Core Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03297)
*Tharindu B. Hewage,Shashikant Ilager,Maria A. Rodriguez,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种软硬件协同设计方法，通过同步多线程(SMT)和深度空闲技术实现服务器负载与可再生能源供需匹配，生成静态逻辑核心集合，从而将低延迟应用纳入跨地理区域的负载迁移框架，在减少80%低延迟虚拟机跨区域迁移的同时，保持可接受的性能折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨地理区域负载迁移技术通过追逐间歇性可再生能源来降低云基础设施碳足迹，但由于广域网(WAN)的高延迟变化，该技术通常无法应用于低延迟应用。因此，需要一种新方法在实现碳减排的同时容纳低延迟应用的需求。

Method: 采用软硬件协同设计方法。硬件层面：通过深度空闲物理核心来匹配可再生能源供应的峰值和谷值，在两个相同服务器池中，一个启用CPU同步多线程(SMT)技术，从而在能源供应动态变化中维持静态逻辑核心集合，减少工作负载迁移概率。软件层面：在区域内为低延迟应用高效追踪静态核心集合，同时优先考虑尽力而为(best-effort)应用的跨WAN迁移需求。利用SMT核心因硬件多线程带来的较低性能折衷特性。基于OpenStack和CPU空闲状态实现该技术。

Result: 在真实实验测试平台上使用Azure虚拟机到达轨迹进行评估。实现：(1)低延迟虚拟机跨区域迁移减少80%；(2)p90终端用户延迟变异系数降低43.81%；(3)由于使用SMT核心导致的最坏情况延迟折衷为11.97%。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计，成功将低延迟应用纳入跨地理区域的负载迁移框架中，在显著降低低延迟应用迁移频率和延迟变化的同时，仅引入较小的性能折衷，为云基础设施的绿色计算提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Load shifting across geographic regions to chase intermittent renewable energy availability is commonly used in reducing cloud infrastructure carbon footprint. However, it often omits low-latency applications due to high latency variances of wide area networks (WAN) that interconnect regions. This paper addresses accommodating low-latency applications into load shifting by minimizing their shifting across the WAN. We propose a technique using a hardware-software co-design approach. At the hardware level, we conduct server load matching over renewables supply peaks and valleys by deep idling physical cores in two otherwise identical server pools, with one enabling simultaneous multi-threading (SMT) in CPUs. In return, we achieve a static set of logical cores amidst energy supply dynamics, reducing the probability of workload shifting. At the software level, we efficiently chase the static set of cores for low-latency applications within regions while prioritizing best-effort applications to accommodate shifting requirements across WANs. Our approach exploits the lower performance compromise of SMT cores due to their hardware multi-threading. We implement the proposed technique with OpenStack and CPU idle states and evaluate its performance on a real experimental testbed with Azure VM arrival traces. Results show an 80% reduction in offloading low-latency VMs and a 43.81% reduction in coefficient of variation of p90 end-user latency while having a worst-case latency compromise of 11.97% due to SMT cores.

</details>


### [100] [Lantern: Finding Committable Transactions via Back-Propagation on DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03315)
*Denglong Li,Gerui Wang,Tian Guan,Mingchao Wan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lantern是一种无需先验知识的确定性并发控制协议，通过识别局部依赖图中零出度事务顶点、引入反向传播机制和无冲突批次选择策略，在ChainMaker区块链平台上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发控制协议存在两个主要问题：要么引入非确定性，导致主节点和副本节点之间存在串行执行-重放依赖；要么依赖不切实际的事务读写集先验知识。这限制了高性能事务处理系统的发展。

Method: 1. 基于局部依赖图识别零出度事务顶点，采用允许覆盖策略按升序安全提交；
2. 提出反向传播机制，从汇点到源点迭代传播依赖状态以识别更多可提交事务；
3. 设计无冲突批次选择（CFBS）策略处理读-修改-写密集场景；
4. 将Lantern集成到开源区块链平台ChainMaker中。

Result: 在YCSB和SmallBank基准测试上的广泛评估表明：Lantern相比Aria实现了最高4.2倍的吞吐量加速；将ChainMaker执行层的吞吐量提升至少2.2倍。

Conclusion: Lantern成功解决了现有并发控制协议的局限性，在无需先验知识的前提下实现了确定性并发控制，显著提升了事务处理系统的性能，特别适用于区块链等高性能场景。

Abstract: Existing concurrency control protocols either introduce nondeterminism, resulting in a serial execution-replay dependency between primary and replica nodes, or rely on impractical prior knowledge of transaction read-write sets. In this paper, we present Lantern, a deterministic concurrency control protocol tailored for high-performance transaction processing systems operating without prior knowledge. The key insight of Lantern is that all zero-out-degree transaction vertices in the local dependency graph can be safely committed in ascending order using an overwrite-permissive strategy. We further introduce a novel Back-Propagation mechanism that iteratively propagates dependency states from sink to source vertices to identify additional committable transactions. We also propose Conflict-Free Batch Selection (CFBS) for read-modify-write intensive scenarios. We integrate Lantern into the open-source blockchain platform ChainMaker. Extensive evaluations on YCSB and SmallBank benchmarks demonstrate that Lantern achieves up to a 4.2x throughput speedup over Aria and improves the throughput of ChainMaker's execution layer by at least 2.2x.

</details>


### [101] [Iapetus: Content-Aware Hierarchical Scheduling for Collaborative ViT Inference in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03318)
*Yan Chen,Yunxiang Zhang,Guanjun Jiang,Haiquan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Sys，一种内容感知的分层调度器，通过令牌压缩和层卸载优化卫星边缘计算中ViT的协同推理，在任务完成率、延迟和能耗方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星边缘计算中ViT的协同推理面临挑战：模型分区产生的大量中间激活数据导致显著传输开销；令牌压缩的质量影响依赖于输入内容、模型深度和先前剪枝决策；层卸载需要适应时变的接触和电池条件。

Method: 提出内容感知的分层调度器Sys：首先筛选星座范围内的选项保留有限候选集，然后通过质量预测和联合规划将每个候选细化为完整的令牌压缩和层卸载轨迹；统一优化目标平衡每任务延迟、能量、质量损失与累积工作负载和电池压力。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin硬件在环测试平台实现并验证；在5任务/秒的负载下，Sys实现91.6%的任务完成率，比最强基线MARATD3高26.1个百分点；平均延迟降低53.0%，电池消耗减少70.8%，同时满足质量目标。

Conclusion: Sys通过内容感知的分层调度有效解决了卫星边缘计算中ViT协同推理的传输开销和资源适应性挑战，在任务完成率、延迟和能耗方面取得显著改进，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Collaborative inference pools distributed resources to run compute-intensive Vision Transformers (ViTs) in satellite edge computing. Model partitioning enables such collaboration by assigning consecutive layer groups to different nodes, but the large volume of intermediate activation data incurs substantial transfer overhead that can erase its benefit. Token compression reduces downstream computation and activation transfer, but its quality impact depends on input content, model depth, and earlier pruning decisions, while layer offloading must adapt to time-varying contact and battery conditions. We present \sys, a content-aware hierarchical scheduler that screens constellation-wide options to retain a bounded candidate set, then refines each candidate into a complete token compression and layer offloading trajectory using quality prediction and joint planning. A unified objective balances per-task latency, energy, and quality loss against accumulated workload and battery pressures. We implement \sys on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin hardware-in-the-loop testbed and use its validated execution model for constellation-scale trace replay across multiple ViT workloads and constellation settings. At \(5\)~tasks/s, \sys accomplishes 91.6\% of released tasks, 26.1 percentage points above MARATD3, the strongest baseline, while reducing mean latency and battery draw by 53.0\% and 70.8\%, respectively, and meeting quality targets.

</details>


### [102] [Latency-Aware Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows on Heterogeneous GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03335)
*Jinghao Wang,Yifeng Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Yao Lu,Yihui Zhang,Xiaoyang Sun,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个预测引导的运行时系统，通过工作流预测构建和优化物理执行图，在异构GPU池上显著降低了端到端完成时间和GPU资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构GPU池上执行并发多智能体工作流时，面临未来依赖关系、服务状态需求、时变负载、模型驻留和资源可用性等复杂挑战。逻辑工作流定义了计算需求，但物理调度单元、模型生命周期动作、资源排序和放置需要根据实时池状态动态选择。

Method: 提出预测引导的运行时系统：(1)预测器估计设备特定的激活延迟、峰值内存和模型加载成本，并通过工作流依赖关系传播预测；(2)构造器构建语义保持的融合和模型生命周期替代方案；(3)调度器基于实时池状态联合优化选择、放置和执行顺序。

Result: 在异构GPU池上的三种工作流场景测试中，与最先进的工作流调度器相比：(1)端到端完成时间减少最多36.8%；(2)突发到达下的P95完成延迟降低最多25.9%；(3)每个完成的会话节省最多24.63 GPU-秒。

Conclusion: 通过将工作流预测与物理执行图构建和优化相结合，该系统能够有效应对异构GPU池上并发多智能体工作流的复杂调度挑战，在性能和资源效率方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Concurrent multi-agent workflows expose future dependencies and serving-state requirements while running on heterogeneous GPU pools with time-varying load, model residency, and resource availability. The logical workflow defines the required computation, whereas its physical scheduling units, model-lifecycle actions, resource ordering, and placement must be selected according to the observed pool state. We present a prediction-guided runtime that uses workflow forecasts to construct and optimize a physical execution graph. Predictor estimates device-specific activation latency, peak memory, and model-loading cost, then propagates these predictions through workflow dependencies to forecast activation readiness and future model demand. Constructor builds semantics-preserving fusion and model-lifecycle alternatives, while Scheduler jointly optimizes their selection, placement, and execution order based on the live pool state. Across a workload spanning three workflow scenarios on a heterogeneous GPU pool, our system reduces end-to-end makespan and overall p95 completion latency under burst arrivals by up to 36.8% and 25.9%, respectively, over state-of-the-art workflow schedulers. It also saves up to 24.63 GPU-s per completed session.

</details>


### [103] [FlowTT: Exploiting Computation Flow Reuse in Irregular Tensor-Train Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03459)
*Jongmin Seok,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlowTT是一种流感知的GPU执行框架，通过识别并利用前缀共享的计算流，显著提升了推荐模型中TT嵌入查找的效率，在Meta基准测试上相比EcoRec延迟降低达42.2%-49.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TT分解方法虽然能有效压缩推荐模型中的大型嵌入表，但TT嵌入查找效率仍然低下。主要瓶颈在于：(1) 不同输入索引间部分共享的计算流未被充分复用；(2) 顺序TT核收缩操作之间的中间结果需要重复地离芯片物化，造成内存访问开销。

Method: FlowTT将TT收集操作重构为一组前缀共享的不规则计算流，并提出三项关键技术：(1) 流对齐的前缀索引分组，最大化共享计算；(2) 融合TT嵌入执行路径，将中间结果保留在片上内存；(3) 持久线程调度机制，结合分块工作窃取和L2检查点技术，在负载倾斜情况下保持复用效率。该方法协同设计了任务形成、数据缓冲和调度策略。

Result: 在Meta合成推荐基准测试（Meta-240、Meta-480和Meta-788）上，FlowTT始终实现最低延迟。在批次大小32768下，相比EcoRec，推理延迟降低达42.2%，训练延迟降低达49.2%，同时达到最低推理峰值内存使用量。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，暴露和利用前缀共享计算是实现高效TT嵌入执行的关键。FlowTT通过流感知的协同设计成功减少了冗余的TT核操作、全局内存流量和负载不平衡问题，为大规模推荐系统中的TT嵌入优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor-Train (TT) decomposition effectively compresses large embedding tables in recommendation models, but TT-based embedding lookup remains inefficient because partially shared computation flows across input indices are not fully reused and intermediate results are repeatedly materialized off-chip between sequential TT-core contractions. We present FlowTT, a flow-aware GPU execution framework that reformulates TT gather as a set of prefix-shared irregular computation flows. FlowTT combines flow-aligned prefix-based index grouping, a fused TT-embedding execution path with on-chip intermediate retention, and persistent-thread scheduling with chunk-based work stealing and L2 checkpointing to preserve reuse under skewed workloads. By co-designing task formation, data buffering, and scheduling with the structure of TT gather, FlowTT reduces redundant TT-core operations, global-memory traffic, and load imbalance. On Meta's synthetic recommendation benchmarks (Meta-240, Meta-480, and Meta-788), FlowTT consistently achieves the lowest latency compared to existing methods. At batch size 32,768, it reduces latency by up to 42.2% in inference and 49.2% in training relative to EcoRec, while also achieving the lowest inference peak memory usage. These results show that exposing prefix-shared computation is key to efficient TT-based embedding execution.

</details>


### [104] [JuPyLive: Seamless Migration of Jupyter Notebook Resources from Laptop to HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03562)
*Sima Attar-Khorasani,Matthias Lieber,Siavash Ghiasvand*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了JuPyLive，一种无需用户干预的Jupyter笔记本迁移机制，实现了本地工作站与远程HPC资源间的无缝切换，用户只需一键操作即可在熟悉的环境中完成迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Jupyter笔记本在本地和远程HPC资源之间迁移时存在复杂性，用户需要学习新语法、进行代码修改和配置，这阻碍了跨学科研究者对HPC集群的使用。需要一种简化迁移流程、保留用户体验的解决方案。

Method: JuPyLive利用ElasticNotebook管理内存状态迁移，自动完成HPC集群上的资源分配，并编排源端和目标端之间的远程通信通道以实现双向迁移。此外，集成HPC状态监控器实时显示可用远程资源。整个过程完全自动化，无需代码修改或配置，通过Jupyter笔记本内的可视化界面一键发起和监控迁移。

Result: JuPyLive成功实现了本地与远程资源之间的无缝迁移，保留了熟悉的Jupyter使用体验；HPC状态监控功能帮助用户做出资源选择决策；无需用户学习新语法或修改代码即可完成迁移操作。

Conclusion: JuPyLive通过桥接本地工作空间与远程HPC资源，以最小用户干预实现了资源密集型工作流的扩展，进一步推动了HPC集群在跨学科研究者中的普及使用。

Abstract: This work introduces JuPyLive, a migration mechanism that enables seamless transition of Jupyter notebooks between local resources of user's workstation and remote resources of high-performance computing~(HPC) environments, while preserving the user experience. JuPyLive eliminates the underlying complexities of migration process, enabling users to freely choose among available local and remote resources, directly within the familiar Jupyter notebook environment via a single click. JuPyLive leverages ElasticNotebook to manage in-memory state migration, it automates resource allocation on HPC cluster and orchestrates required remote communication channels between the source and destination to enable a bidirectional migration. Furthermore, HPC status monitor of JuPyLive provides a live overview of available remote resources, allowing users to make informed decisions on choosing the relevant resources before initiating a migration process. The proposed fully automatic mechanism requires no code changes or configurations by the end user, nor does it demand users to learn a new syntax, instead the migration process can be intuitively initiated and monitored using visual elements from within the Jupyter notebook. By bridging the gap between local workspace and remote resources, JuPyLive offers a seamless experience for scaling local resource-intensive workflows with minimal user intervention, thus further democratizing the usage of HPC clusters among the interdisciplinary researchers.

</details>


### [105] [Every Kernel Is a Join: Automatic Multi-GPU Parallelism for AI Computations in Einsummable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03905)
*Zhimin Ding,Chen-Kuan Liao,Chima Adiole,Brianna Barrow,Fangzhou Du,Yu Hsiao,Ge Huang,Yicheng Jin,Ismail Syed,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Einsummable是一个自动分布式AI计算系统，无需用户指定设备分配或通信操作，在八卡A100服务器上运行LLaMA transformer块的性能优于手工调优的PyTorch和vLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU服务器上的AI计算分布式部署是AI系统领域的核心问题。现有方法需要程序员手动指定设备分配、分片注解和通信操作，缺乏自动化且难以发现最优并行策略。

Method: 将每个操作建模为关系连接后接张量关系聚合，通过"join-agg specs"暴露可能的分解方式。优化器搜索全局计算的最优分解以最小化通信代价，使用编译时合成的拓扑感知交换程序实现，不依赖预定义的集合通信原语。

Result: 在八卡A100服务器上运行LLaMA transformer块，Einsummable达到8.97ms几何平均运行时间，优于手工调优PyTorch的13.80ms和vLLM的15.90ms。系统能发现基于网格的自动并行器无法发现的执行计划。

Conclusion: 通过将操作统一建模为连接-聚合并搜索分解空间而非命名策略，Einsummable实现了完全自动的多GPU分布式计算，性能超越手工设计的实现，证明了基于关系模型的编译时优化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Distributing an AI computation across the GPUs of a multi-GPU server is one of the central problems in systems-for-AI. We present Einsummable, a prototype system that accepts a PyTorch-like description of an AI computation and automatically distributes it across a multi-GPU server, with no device assignments, sharding annotations, or communication operations written by the programmer. Einsummable models every operation as a relational join followed by an aggregation over tensor relations, in which the tuples contain sub-tensors. Each operation exposes its possible decompositions through what we call "join-agg specs". An optimizer then selects decompositions across the whole computation to minimize a communication-cost proxy. Because it searches decompositions rather than a menu of named strategies, Einsummable discovers plans that mesh-based auto-parallelizers cannot. Each decomposed operation is implemented by synthesizing an exchange program, which is a topology-aware generalization of Volcano's exchange operator. Einsummable invokes no canned collectives: all communication and aggregation is special-purpose, derived at compile time. Despite being fully automatic, Einsummable can outperform custom-designed implementations. For example, on LLaMA transformer blocks on an eight-GPU A100 server, Einsummable achieves a geometric-mean runtime of 8.97 ms, versus 13.80 ms for hand-tuned PyTorch and 15.90 ms for vLLM.

</details>


### [106] [Barnacle: Adaptive Multi-Leader Scheduling for DAG-Based Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03978)
*Zeno De Angeli,Alexandru Ianov Vitanov,Philipp Jovanovic,Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino,Pasindu Tennage,Igor Zablotchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Barnacle自适应领导者数量调整机制，解决了DAG共识中多领导者导致的队头阻塞问题，在健康网络中降低6-13%延迟，在退化网络中保持单领导者稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG共识中多领导者可降低排队延迟，但实际部署仅使用单领导者，原因在于队头阻塞问题：慢速领导者会阻塞流水线至少一个超时周期，且该风险随领导者数量增加而增大。

Method: Barnacle是一种运行时自适应调整领导者数量的插件机制。定期测量已提交DAG上通过直接规则决定的槽位比例，采用加性增、乘性减策略动态调整领导者数量。测量无需额外消息和密码学操作，具有确定性，且可泛化应用于多种DAG协议。

Result: 在四种涵盖拜占庭(3f+1, 5f+1)、崩溃(2c+1)及混合(5f+3c+1)故障模型的协议上验证了安全性和活性。健康网络中延迟比单领导者降低6-13%；退化网络中匹配单领导者性能，同时比静态高领导者数量降低35-56%延迟。

Conclusion: Barnacle成功解决了多领导者队头阻塞问题，实现了动态最优领导者数量选择，目前已与Sui区块链团队合作进行集成。

Abstract: In DAG-based consensus, all validators propose blocks concurrently, and designated leader blocks drive transaction commit. Having multiple leader slots per round cuts queuing latency, yet production deployments run a single leader because of head-of-line blocking: a slow leader stalls the pipeline for at least one leader timeout, and for several waves when its slot must wait for the fallback indirect decision rule. This risk grows with the leader count. We introduce Barnacle, an add-on that adapts the leader count at run time. Every interval, it measures on the agreed committed DAG the fraction of slots decided as commit by the direct rule, and drives the leader count with additive increase, multiplicative decrease. The measurement requires no extra messages and no cryptography, and is deterministic. Barnacle is generic over DAG protocols; we instantiate it on four protocols spanning the Byzantine (3f + 1, 5f + 1), crash-only (2c + 1), and mixed (5f + 3c + 1) fault models, with proven safety and liveness.
